<p> Montreal Gazette - RSS Feedhttps : **30;2050;TOOLONG , 27 Mar 2020 00:52:01 +0000Coronavirus : Montreal 's vaunted summer festivals in doubthttp : **35;2082;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;2119;TOOLONG ... title= " Osheaga 2019 . Will current social-distancing sensitivities have an impact when life returns to normal ? " alt= " Osheaga 2019 . Will current social-distancing sensitivities have an impact when life returns to normal ? " data-portal-copyright= " John Kenney " **28;2154;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1759169 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;2184;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; No one knows whether Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s big summer festivals are going to happen this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many major international music festivals and concert tours have been postponed or cancelled because of the COVID-19 crisis , and Evenko &amp;mdash ; Quebec&amp;rsquo ; s leading concert promoter &amp;mdash ; announced March 13 it was cancelling all of its shows for the next 30 days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But will the Montreal International Jazz Festival , Just For Laughs and Osheaga take place as usual ? Industry insiders say it&amp;rsquo ; s simply too early to tell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everyone is on pause , &amp;rdquo ; said Laurent Saulnier , vice-president of programming at L&amp;rsquo ; &amp;Eacute ; quipe Spectra , which runs the jazz fest and Les Francos&amp;nbsp ; de Montr&amp;eacute ; al &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re just waiting to see when things will change in terms of people having to be confined to their homes and not being allowed to congregate . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Les Francos is supposed to run June 12-21 and the jazz fest is June 25-July 4 . Osheaga is skedded for July 31-Aug. 2. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Live-event companies are laying off large portions of their workforces as a result of the COVID-19 cancellations . On Thursday , the Just For Laughs Group , which runs the English-language festival and its francophone counterpart , Juste Pour Rire , laid off 75 per cent of its employees . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just For Laughs could not be reached for comment , and in a statement did not reveal how many people were being laid off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div class= " page " title= " Page 1 " &gt; &lt;div class= " section " &gt; &lt;div class= " layoutArea " &gt; &lt;div class= " column " &gt; &lt;p&gt; " As we are faced with the global situation evolving so quickly , it continues to have profound effect on our industry and our business , " president Charles D ? carie said in a statement . " As such , we have had to take all necessary measures to stabilize our situation and ensure the sustainability of our business activities . " &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;On Tuesday , &lt;a href= " https : **33;2220;TOOLONG ... CH&lt;/a&gt; -- which owns the Montreal Canadiens , Evenko and L ' ? quipe Spectra -- announced it was laying off 60 per cent of its staff . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Last week , Montreal-based &lt;a href= " https : **33;2255;TOOLONG ... du Soleil&lt;/a&gt; gave pink slips to 95 per cent of its employees following the cancellation of all of its shows around the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;p&gt; The majority of tours scheduled for March , April and May have been cancelled or postponed , but many of the summer tours are still set to go &amp;mdash ; in theory . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foo Fighters have postponed a bunch of spring dates , and Bon Iver and Harry Styles have pushed spring 2020 dates in Europe into 2021. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; C&amp;eacute ; line Dion has postponed the March and April dates of her Courage world tour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Bonnaroo Music and Arts Festival in Tennessee , which was set to happen June 11-14 , has been rescheduled for Sept. 24-27. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Coachella Valley Music and Arts Festival , the major Californian music fest set to take place in April , has been pushed back to October . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Promoters not only don&amp;rsquo ; t know when government regulations will change , but also what the mood of the public will be once the stay-at-home rules and quarantine measures are lifted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a double-edged sword , &amp;rdquo ; Saulnier said . &amp;ldquo ; People are going to have a real hunger to go out . They&amp;rsquo ; ll have been stuck in their houses for weeks and weeks . So to go out and see people , to have a few beers , that&amp;rsquo ; ll be something they&amp;rsquo ; ve been waiting weeks for . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But the other side of the coin is people are beginning to live in a world of social distancing . So what will be the impact of that in the coming months and years ? Your guess is as good as mine . I just know that there are some places where it will be impossible to maintain social distancing . In any cinema or theatre , you won&amp;rsquo ; t be able to have that kind of distance between people . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spectra and Evenko have already cancelled dozens of shows . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Festivals in April and May are already completely cancelled and June is probably next , and no one is rescheduling anything until September , &amp;rdquo ; said Dan Seligman , creative director at the POP Montreal music&amp;nbsp ; festival , which is set to run Sept. 23-27. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everyone has to wait for directives from the authorities , &amp;rdquo ; Seligman said . &amp;ldquo ; Also the border is closed . As of right now , given the current protocols and laws , nothing is happening . Will those get lifted in May ? I feel like it&amp;rsquo ; s too early to say 100 per cent about anything . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;2290;TOOLONG " **34;2319;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **32;2355;TOOLONG " &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;2389;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;2431;TOOLONG ... NewsNewsCOVID-19 : Uncertainty takes the stage for Montreal theatrehttp : **33;2874;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;2909;TOOLONG ... title= " Centaur Theatre had to cut short its presentation of the thriller Mob , starring Adrianne Richards . " The best thing that people can do is not ask for their money back , " Centaur boss Eda Holmes says of the devastating loss of box office revenue during the COVID-19 crisis . She hopes patrons will consider " just letting the ticket live as a tribute to the arts in Canada . We 'll happily give them a tax receipt . " " alt= " Centaur Theatre had to cut short its presentation of the thriller Mob , starring Adrianne Richards . " The best thing that people can do is not ask for their money back , " Centaur boss Eda Holmes says of the devastating loss of box office revenue during the COVID-19 crisis . She hopes patrons will consider " just letting the ticket live as a tribute to the arts in Canada . We 'll happily give them a tax receipt . " " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;2944;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1758027 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **32;2974;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not a time to be falling prey to superstitions and bad omens , but it does seem grimly appropriate that &lt;a href= " https : **33;3008;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Centaur Theatre 's critically acclaimed , massively successful production of Mob&lt;/a&gt; had to close on Friday the 13th , amid the COVID-19 crisis . Theatre folk across Montreal , as across the world , may well be knocking on wood for better times , but hope of succour from funding bodies like &lt;a href= " https : //www.calq.gouv.qc.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;CALQ&lt;/a&gt; and the &lt;a href= " https : //canadacouncil.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Canada Council for the Arts&lt;/a&gt; would be a more realistic lifeline out of the limbo of uncertainty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;3043;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;A 30-day shutdown period announced in mid-March&lt;/a&gt; is looking more and more optimistic . Where that leaves the city&amp;rsquo ; s major theatre companies , countless independents and forthcoming festivals like the &lt;a href= " https : //montrealfringe.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Montreal Fringe&lt;/a&gt; and the recently announced 14th edition of &lt;a href= " https : //fta.ca/en/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Festival TransAm ? riques&lt;/a&gt; is now anybody&amp;rsquo ; s guess . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the time of writing ( a qualifying phrase that should be taken as read from now on ) , theatre companies are waiting to hear concrete proposals from CALQ and the Canada Council , but the auguries are at least encouraging . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; No details have yet come through , but they have promised to help us , &amp;rdquo ; &lt;a href= " https : //centaurtheatre.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Centaur&lt;/a&gt; boss Eda Holmes told the Montreal Gazette by phone . &amp;ldquo ; There have been meetings and we&amp;rsquo ; ve gotten information that something is coming , but we don&amp;rsquo ; t know what . They&amp;rsquo ; ve only said that we need to keep track of the losses incurred from this situation . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Government subsidies and sponsorship are obviously crucial , but the most devastating hit theatres have been taking in this crisis is the loss of box office revenue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The best thing that people can do is not ask for their money back , &amp;rdquo ; says Holmes , hoping that patrons will consider &amp;ldquo ; just letting the ticket live as a tribute to the arts in Canada . We&amp;rsquo ; ll happily give them a tax receipt . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over at the &lt;a href= " https : **29;3078;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Segal Centre&lt;/a&gt; , where the stirring tribute to &amp;lsquo ; 60s protest songs &lt;a href= " https : **33;3109;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Times They Are A Changin ' &lt;/a&gt; had its run cut short , along with Teesri Duniya Theatre&amp;rsquo ; s powerful play Counter Offence in the Segal Studio , artistic director Lisa Rubin is holding out a similar hope . Like Holmes , she&amp;rsquo ; s aware of the awkwardness of asking patrons to make financial sacrifices at this time . Still , both have been buoyed by the results so far . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We just got a recent report , and the number of donations far outweighs the number of refunds , &amp;rdquo ; says Rubin. &amp;ldquo ; There will be ramifications , there will still be a huge loss , but there is a great feeling of &amp;lsquo ; we&amp;rsquo ; re here to help you too . &amp;rsquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s both a sad feeling and one of humanity coming together . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;3144;TOOLONG ... title= " In addition to curtailing the run of The Times They Are A Changin ' , starring W. Joseph Matheson and Louise Pitre , the Segal Centre had to cancel the opening date of its next production . Artistic director Lisa Rubin says government orders have " enabled so many of us to ( use ) the Act of God or force majeure clauses that you never think you 'll need " when dealing with insurance companies . " alt= " In addition to curtailing the run of The Times They Are A Changin ' , starring W. Joseph Matheson and Louise Pitre , the Segal Centre had to cancel the opening date of its next production . Artistic director Lisa Rubin says government orders have " enabled so many of us to ( use ) the Act of God or force majeure clauses that you never think you 'll need " when dealing with insurance companies . " data-portal-copyright= " Joanna Akyol " **28;3179;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1758030 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As well as word from the funding bodies , theatre companies are waiting on decisions from their insurers . There&amp;rsquo ; s no small measure of relief in the fact that the government quickly moved from shutting down theatres that accommodate audiences of 250-plus to &lt;a href= " https : **33;3209;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;shutting down all theatres&lt;/a&gt; whatever their capacities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 250-plus rule meant unambiguous curtains for shows like Cats at Place des Arts and TNM&amp;rsquo ; s Les 3 Soeurs . But it left companies like Centaur ( seating for Mob was around 240 ) , let alone much smaller spaces like MainLine Theatre and the MAI , responsible for deciding whether the show must go on &amp;mdash ; not a good place to be when negotiating with insurance companies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rubin says those orders from above have &amp;ldquo ; enabled so many of us to ( use ) the Act of God or force majeure clauses that you never think you&amp;rsquo ; ll need . The COVID-19 pandemic is forcing institutions to shut down due to an unprecedented event that is no fault of theirs . It means we&amp;rsquo ; re all in this together . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Theatres were originally slated to reopen in April , pending a reassessment , but that now seems highly unlikely . And even if theatres were to open around that time , that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t mean their seasons will proceed as planned . On the day I spoke to Rubin , the Segal was officially cancelling the April 19 opening date of its production of Oslo , J.T. Rogers&amp;rsquo ; s epic exploration of the 1993 Middle East peace talks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s simply too soon for rehearsals to begin next week , &amp;rdquo ; says Rubin. &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re not even allowed to have 30 people together . ( Of course , by now , no non-essential gathering of people is possible . ) And with a vulnerable population as our core audience , it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t seem the way to go at this point . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , Rubin continues , with a perhaps forced note of cheerfulness , they&amp;rsquo ; re postponing rather than cancelling : &amp;ldquo ; I can say Oslo will rise again . I just don&amp;rsquo ; t know when . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;3244;TOOLONG ... title= " Place des Arts had been scheduled to present Cats this month . " alt= " Place des Arts had been scheduled to present Cats this month . " data-portal-copyright= " Montreal Gazette files " **28;3279;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1758031 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Centaur&amp;rsquo ; s next show , a co-production with Black Theatre Workshop of August Wilson&amp;rsquo ; s Fences that was scheduled to open April 21 , has been cancelled by mutual decision of both companies . As if the heartache at Centaur weren&amp;rsquo ; t enough , Holmes is also dealing with the unknowns of this year&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;3309;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Shaw Festival&lt;/a&gt; , for which she&amp;rsquo ; s supposed to be directing The Devil&amp;rsquo ; s Disciple for a May opening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One recurring suggestion for keeping theatre alive during this crisis is the possibility of online productions , though as things currently stand , the logistics of that simply don&amp;rsquo ; t add up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Maybe the Met opera could do that , maybe they&amp;rsquo ; re set up for it , but not the Centaur , &amp;rdquo ; says Holmes . &amp;ldquo ; Creating something worth watching is exceedingly expensive . And it still means putting actors together . At this moment , we really have to listen to what public health officials are telling us and stay home . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet some modest at-a-distance initiatives are proceeding and are likely to multiply . &lt;a href= " https : //www.rideauvert.qc.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Th ? ? tre du Rideau Vert&lt;/a&gt; , for instance , adapted its production of Strindberg&amp;rsquo ; s Mademoiselle Julie for radio broadcast on March 19 . Toronto playwright Nick Green launched the online &lt;a href= " https : **33;3344;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Social Distancing Festival&lt;/a&gt; , featuring artists from around the world . And Festival TransAm&amp;eacute ; riques &lt;a href= " https : **33;3379;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;live-streamed its announcement&lt;/a&gt; of this year&amp;rsquo ; s lineup on Facebook. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;3414;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;On Thursday the FTA announced&lt;/a&gt; that , because of federal restrictions on travel , the 12 shows representing the international component of the festival have been cancelled . For now , the 10 shows from Canada and Quebec are still scheduled to take place from May 20 to June 3. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite all the unknowns in these scary times , it feels appropriate to end this piece on these clear-eyed yet hopeful , fearful yet galvanizing words from FTA director Martin Faucher , from a speech he made during that Facebook event : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This 14th edition of Festival TransAm&amp;eacute ; riques takes place at the beginning of a new decade that will be crucial to humanity , now on the verge of collapse . ( Faucher also referenced environmental catastrophes . ) This festival is a celebration , a respite that shakes up collective apathy . Standing together , united by art , we can do a lot . Let us use our strength . To quote the renowned Peter Brook ( one of the international artists who had been scheduled to take part in the FTA ) : &amp;lsquo ; Theatre is life . &amp;rsquo ; Be inspired by this dynamic 95-year-old artist . Let us engage with life , for the sky is still there , an astonishing marvel . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;3449;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;3491;TOOLONG ... of our coronavirus **29;3526;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;3557;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;5697;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1753471 " /&gt; Denise Duguay , Montreal GazetteSat , 21 Mar 2020 00:01:47 **45;5727;TOOLONG ... Reichs 's latest Temperance Brennan novel is rooted in a personal setbackhttp : **33;5774;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;5809;TOOLONG ... title= " " I 'm almost never asked to explain forensic anthropology anymore . There is a global awareness of the expertise , " says part-time Montrealer Kathy Reichs . " Perhaps 12 seasons of Bones and 19 Temperance Brennan novels helped just a wee bit to create that awareness . " " alt= " " I 'm almost never asked to explain forensic anthropology anymore . There is a global awareness of the expertise , " says part-time Montrealer Kathy Reichs . " Perhaps 12 seasons of Bones and 19 Temperance Brennan novels helped just a wee bit to create that awareness . " " data-portal-copyright= " Marie-Reine Mattera " **28;5844;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1745556 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;When forensic anthropologist Kathy Reichs created forensic anthropologist Temperance Brennan in 1997 , a crime fiction dynasty of rare richness and enormous popularity was set in motion . Twenty books and an acclaimed spinoff TV series later comes A Conspiracy of Bones ( Simon &amp; Schuster , 432 pages , $24.99 ) , in which Brennan finds herself a professional lone wolf , at loggerheads with an intransigent boss . She is also getting older in real time , with the frailties and setbacks age can entail -- some of them shared with her creator . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Reichs , who spends part of each year in Montreal , was interviewed by email from her home in Charlotte , N.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;A Conspiracy of Bones begins with an epigraph&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;from Virginia Woolf : " It is far harder to kill a phantom than a reality . " What 's the significance of it ? **28;5874;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The theme of the book concerns what information is real versus what is fake . On two levels . Because of health issues , and because Tempe is robbed of all of her hard data at one point in the story , she has to determine what is true with regard to what is stored in her head . Can she trust her own perceptions and recollections , the only intel left to her ? &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;On a broader level , people are living in a time when " fake news " and " alternative facts " have become common lingo . In an atmosphere in which the average viewer , listener and reader is constantly bombarded with information , some true , some false . Anyone can get on the air or on the internet and promote any theory he or she chooses , no matter how dangerous or absurd . Distortions ( are ) coming from people in positions of authority . With so much misinformation being disseminated , it 's hard to kill the " phantom " once it 's out there . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Temperance Brennan has been bumping up against some physical challenges , including a cerebral aneurysm and subsequent surgery . Of all the things she might have been subject to , why did you choose that ? **28;5904;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;As with all of my novels , I take actual cases and experiences , then tweak the details to create a fictional storyline . But I 've always been reluctant to reveal details of my personal life . With this book I resolved to change that . I decided to share with my readers a challenge I 've faced recently . Not long ago I was diagnosed with an unruptured cerebral aneurysm . After undergoing surgery , I am fine . The bottom line : I have a brain oddity and headaches , so our heroine also has the dastardly duo . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;How significant is it that Temperance finds herself unable to work through an official facility ? Does this entail a radical change in her working method ? **28;5934;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;It definitely does . For the first time , she ca n't rely on the support of personnel or technology provided by her affiliation with a medical examiner office or forensic lab . She has to draw on her own network of colleagues . And on her own resources . From a practical point of view , it 's very difficult for her to be an outsider . From a psychological standpoint , doubly so . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " Faceless " is a word generally used to denote anonymity and the sense of being lost in a crowd . The body in A Conspiracy of Bones is literally faceless . Should we read any symbolism into this ? And for the layperson , how much of a hurdle is the lack of a face in forensics ? **28;5964;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;No facial features : no visual ID . No teeth : no dental records . No hands : no fingerprints . A corpse lacking identifiers -- that should be an anthropology case , yet Tempe 's expertise is not requested . For her , the faceless man represents all the nameless dead she is dedicated to naming , all the unresolved deaths she is committed to resolving . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;What is your work ratio these days ? Do your practice and your writing coexist in a structured way ? **28;5994;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;At one point not so long ago I was writing a screenplay , a YA book and a Temperance Brennan novel each year . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt;As there is only so much time in the day , that meant something had to go . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt;That something was casework . I still maintain my board certification ( American Board of Forensic Anthropology ) . I attend the annual meeting of my main professional organization ( American Academy of Forensic Sciences ) . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt;I stay current with the latest methodology and technology by reading professional journals . Though I am available should my expertise be needed , I no longer go to any lab with regularity . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;6024;TOOLONG ... title= " Emily Deschanel as Temperance Brennan in Bones . " alt= " Emily Deschanel as Temperance Brennan in Bones . " data-portal-copyright= " Frank Ockenfels " **28;6059;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1745563 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;At this point in your career , do you find that there is a broader awareness of what forensic anthropology is than when you started ? **28;6089;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Absolutely . I 'm almost never asked to explain forensic anthropology anymore . There is a global awareness of the expertise . Back in the ' 90s , when I started writing fiction , that was definitely not the case . Perhaps 12 seasons of Bones and 19 Temperance Brennan novels helped just a wee bit to create that awareness . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Are you aware of people who have taken up forensic anthropology through your inspiration ? **28;6119;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;I love when readers or viewers approach me at signings or other events and tell me they have entered the field of forensic anthropology because they were inspired by Temperance Brennan . I am told by colleagues in academia that forensic science programs around the world have come into existence or been expanded because of increased demand by students . Back in 1994 when I began my first novel , D ? j ? Dead , my goal was to bring my field to a broader audience . I hope I may have inspired a few future scientists . Especially those of the female persuasion ! &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Is Bones officially over ? Do you have other adaptation irons in the fire ? **28;6149;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;With 246 episodes , Bones is the longest-running scripted drama in the history of Fox . But the time comes when all good things must end . The cast , crew , producers and writers have moved on and are involved in other projects . A reunion is always possible . I would love that&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; . But for now , you will have to catch Brennan , Booth and the squints on reruns. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Last year I was busy pitching , with a partner , an idea for an original scripted drama . And I 'm engaged in very early-stage talks about the possibility of a project involving my&lt;i&gt; &lt;/i&gt;Virals series&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; . Who knows ? Hollywood is a fickle business . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;6179;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;6210;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;6248;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;6290;TOOLONG ... NewsQ&amp;amp ; A : Grimes talks about art , pregnancy , politics and boyfriend Elon Muskhttp : **36;7139;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;7177;TOOLONG ... title= " Canadian musician Grimes ( Claire Boucher ) performs in 2016 Osheaga at Jean-Drapeau Park in Montreal . " alt= " Canadian musician Grimes ( Claire Boucher ) performs in 2016 Osheaga at Jean-Drapeau Park in Montreal . " data-portal-copyright= " Dario Ayala " **28;7212;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1749244 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;7242;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //www.grimesmusic.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Grimes&lt;/a&gt; has perhaps become more famous for her controversial remarks and being in a relationship with billionaire Elon Musk than her music , and if that&amp;rsquo ; s indeed the case , that&amp;rsquo ; s a real shame because Claire Boucher &amp;mdash ; who originally hails from Vancouver and got her indie music career going while living in Montreal &amp;mdash ; makes some of the most unique music on the planet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her 2012 album , Visions , really got the global ball rolling but it was 2015&amp;rsquo ; s Art Angels that really set the Grimes template of somehow mixing an extreme alt-culture sensibility with surprisingly accessible sounds . At the start , it was easy to describe Grimes&amp;rsquo ; s work as electronic , but with her latest album , &lt;a href= " https : **32;7278;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Miss Anthropocene&lt;/a&gt; , it&amp;rsquo ; s become impossible to define her sound . In a recent interview from her home in Los Angeles , she said that&amp;rsquo ; s exactly the way she likes it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;7312;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;7348;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Montreal Gazette : &lt;/strong&gt; How are you ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; Not bad . Well , actually , I&amp;rsquo ; m very pregnant . I&amp;rsquo ; m very slow and very tired . I&amp;rsquo ; m slow as f&amp;mdash ; so do not judge . My brain is like 10 per cent down . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; How is being pregnant impacting you ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s just the last little bit . Mostly it&amp;rsquo ; s been OK . At the beginning it was hard . I feel much more sympathy for my mom now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; How many weeks pregnant are you ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; 31. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7384;TOOLONG ... title= " Grimes has confirmed that Elon Musk , seen with her here at the 2018 Met Gala in NYC , is the father of her baby . " alt= " Grimes has confirmed that Elon Musk , seen with her here at the 2018 Met Gala in NYC , is the father of her baby . " data-portal-copyright= " ANGELA WEISS " **28;7419;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1749316 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; What has it meant for your music ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; Productivity is down but ideas are up , which is interesting . I have tons and tons of ideas but I&amp;rsquo ; m just not executing them very well . But I&amp;rsquo ; m getting a lot of sh&amp;ndash ; on paper , which is great . It&amp;rsquo ; s just a stamina issue really and singing is hard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; Will you become one of these artists who writes endless songs about being a parent ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; No . I don&amp;rsquo ; t know if that&amp;rsquo ; s artistically compelling to people . It&amp;rsquo ; s not even compelling to me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe src= " https : **34;7449;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; How has the process of making music changed for you in recent years ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; In a lot of ways it is the same . Music for me is kind of like this weird meditative state/chill state . I typically work alone . I lock myself in a room and it&amp;rsquo ; s a chill zone . But as you become increasingly in the public eye , it becomes more intense obviously . But the actual function of making music retains this nice quality for me like it was in the beginning . And it&amp;rsquo ; s important for me to preserve that . I think the main difference is I&amp;rsquo ; d probably drop a lot more music if the pressure wasn&amp;rsquo ; t so high . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7485;TOOLONG ... title= " Grimes in 2012 at the Full Flex Express , an electronic music festival at Jean Drapeau Park . " alt= " Grimes in 2012 at the Full Flex Express , an electronic music festival at Jean Drapeau Park . " data-portal-copyright= " Tijana Martin " **28;7520;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1749256 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; Do you think about the balance between making music that is commercial and following your creative urges ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; I feel like I&amp;rsquo ; m luckily in a weird position where people don&amp;rsquo ; t expect me to be making radio hits . I&amp;rsquo ; m thinking &amp;lsquo ; How do I become Radiohead&amp;rsquo ; not &amp;lsquo ; How do I become Taylor Swift&amp;rsquo ; . The thing I&amp;rsquo ; m aiming at personally is just great immortal art , if I can get there . I kind of love that goal . I don&amp;rsquo ; t presume that I&amp;rsquo ; m ever getting there . Especially with Miss Anthropocene , I was just like &amp;ndash ; I want to make something f&amp;mdash ; ing crazy . The main goal is to make something really different from anything else . That&amp;rsquo ; s like jarring and emotional as opposed to making a hit , which one day I hope to do . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; Did your years in Montreal have a big influence on you ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; I think Montreal was incredibly essential to my development as an artist . I can&amp;rsquo ; t speak to the vibe there currently but when I was coming up , the scene was thriving like crazy . A lot of people were showing me how to do things . I was playing a lot of shows . I played so many shows locally before I got on the road . Most artists when they break , they haven&amp;rsquo ; t played a lot of shows&amp;hellip ; Montreal , you can play 100 shows before you go play in other cities . There&amp;rsquo ; s such a focus on creativity and experimentalism . I think that scenes can be really important and helpful and amazing . It&amp;rsquo ; s a really cool thing . Like Arcade Fire introduced me to a lot of people I still work with . There&amp;rsquo ; s this cool mentoring aspect of things and most cities don&amp;rsquo ; t have that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;7550;TOOLONG ... title= " Grimes in 2000. " alt= " Grimes in 2000. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;7585;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1749238 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; What was your reaction when some of your fans objected to &lt;a href= " https : **33;7615;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;your relationship with Elon Musk&lt;/a&gt; ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; I understand it . It&amp;rsquo ; s just part of the thing . I respect people&amp;rsquo ; s opinions and I get where people are coming from . I respectfully disagree . There&amp;rsquo ; s been a lot of sensationalistic stories that paint things in a different light than they actually are , but people are mad because they care about the world being better and they misunderstand what my boyfriend does . I respect that the impulse is coming from a good place . They&amp;rsquo ; re mad because they believe in certain things and I can&amp;rsquo ; t really criticize that . I think my boyfriend works really f&amp;mdash ; ing hard to fix a lot of our environmental issues but I totally get that some people don&amp;rsquo ; t approve of how he&amp;rsquo ; s doing that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;7650;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;7686;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MG : &lt;/strong&gt; What&amp;rsquo ; s your take on American politics as a Canadian in L.A. ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grimes : &lt;/strong&gt; I really appreciated growing up having access to good health care and I didn&amp;rsquo ; t go into major debt by going to college . And I think those things make Canada better . Canadians tend to be , on average , compared to Americans , a lot less angry and in a lot less pain . I don&amp;rsquo ; t know any Canadians who&amp;rsquo ; re $400,000 in debt because of a medical problem or school . And that makes life better when there isn&amp;rsquo ; t some sh&amp;ndash ; hanging over your head . It leads to a better mental state in society in general . There&amp;rsquo ; s just a lot less hatred and a lot less anger . So I think it&amp;rsquo ; s cool that certain candidates are proposing more Canadian-like ways of living . A lot of Americans say it&amp;rsquo ; s impossible but I say &amp;mdash ; look at Canada . Obviously it&amp;rsquo ; s not perfect but it does seem to be a better living standard than in America and I think it leads to a more peaceful society . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;7722;TOOLONG " **34;7751;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **32;7787;TOOLONG " &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;7821;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;7863;TOOLONG ... plans to ' kill off ' stage **25;7898;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;7925;TOOLONG ... You 're Gon na Rire tonight and it wo n't even cost youhttp : **37;8071;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;8110;TOOLONG ... title= " Sugar Sammy : " We 're going to have great stories to tell our grandchildren . " " alt= " Sugar Sammy : " We 're going to have great stories to tell our grandchildren . " " data-portal-copyright= " Dave Sidaway " **28;8145;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1750804 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;8175;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nothing to do ? Nowhere to go ? Coronavirus blues bumming you out ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; How about a free Sugar Sammy fix and some chuckles therapy in the process ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sugar Sammy&amp;rsquo ; s mega-hit Franglais offering You&amp;rsquo ; re Gon na Rire &amp;mdash ; filmed during its extended run at L&amp;rsquo ; Olympia theatre &amp;mdash ; will stream until Friday night on the comic&amp;rsquo ; s Facebook page . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div class= " fb-video " data-allowfullscreen= " true " data-href= " https : **28;8211;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Restez chez vous and take care of yourself , &amp;rdquo ; Sugar Sammy has urged . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I haven&amp;rsquo ; t showered in three days , but my hands are cleaner than they&amp;rsquo ; ve ever been , &amp;rdquo ; he added on a Twitter post . &amp;ldquo ; Don&amp;rsquo ; t forget social distancing . Yes , it&amp;rsquo ; s annoying , but we&amp;rsquo ; re going to have great stories to tell our grandchildren . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As is the case with pretty much most live entertainers , these have also been tough times for Sugar Sammy . He just had 10 shows cancelled that were slated to run at the Casino de Paris beginning this week and several others postponed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Living and working in France has been , needless to say , something of a nightmare . Much as it has been throughout most of Europe and as it is fast becoming on this continent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We can all use a few laughs now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You&amp;rsquo ; re Gon na Rire is more than a hysterical comedy spectacle . It is a classic take on the unique Montreal multi-cultural experience . Dare we say , quite the sociological experience , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sammy may be occasionally lewd and crude and stoop to stereotypes . But he gets away with it . He is , after all , a Bill 101 poster boy . A child of immigrants , he attended French primary and secondary school and his mastery of the language of Moli&amp;egrave ; re is not questioned . He is equally comfortable performing in French as in English , not to mention in Hindi and Punjabi as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like so many other Montrealers , he sees the world through a multicultural lens . And he loves it . As he points out in his show , this is the only city in the world where you will hear someone utter English , French and Greek in a single sentence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While he embraces the richness and diversity of the city in the show , he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t hold back in pointing out some of the culture shock he experienced . Such as being taken to a cabane &amp;agrave ; sucre as a kid : &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know what to do . Was I supposed to smoke that tree or stick my fingers up its trunk ? &amp;hellip ; ( maple sugar ) is the only food I know that looks better coming out of your body than going in . And I&amp;rsquo ; m Indian , so we&amp;rsquo ; ve seen some horrible-looking stuff coming out . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Veteran standup Joey Elias is coping as best he can in these trying times , despite the fact he&amp;rsquo ; s had close to 25 gigs cancelled . Now , he&amp;rsquo ; s down to a spot on Global TV Friday mornings at 6:40. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But I&amp;rsquo ; ve been prepping for this my whole life , &amp;rdquo ; Elias said . &amp;ldquo ; As someone with anxiety , I&amp;rsquo ; ve always been living as a self-isolated individual . So welcome to my world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thus Elias feels qualified to explain what we&amp;rsquo ; re doing wrong in this COVID-19 crisis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; First , toilet paper &amp;mdash ; if you can find it &amp;mdash ; is not the cure . We&amp;rsquo ; re hoarding it like it&amp;rsquo ; s currency . They&amp;rsquo ; re not smokes and we&amp;rsquo ; re not in prison . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And apparently none of us did well in pre-kindergarten , because all there was to remember then was to wash your hands , don&amp;rsquo ; t touch your face and don&amp;rsquo ; t touch your friend&amp;rsquo ; s face . But apparently we forgot all that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Elias is trying to remain positive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Virtually no comics , or any live entertainers for that matter , are working now , &amp;rdquo ; Elias added . &amp;ldquo ; But we&amp;rsquo ; re all in the same boat . I&amp;rsquo ; m walking down Ste-Catherine St. now and nothing is open and few are making money . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But it&amp;rsquo ; s just a matter of waiting this out . Then we&amp;rsquo ; re all going to need a really good laugh , and not from our living rooms . So in the interim , I&amp;rsquo ; ll just catch up on my laundry . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; St. Paddy&amp;rsquo ; s weekend has traditionally been one of the highlights of award-winning standup Derek Seguin&amp;rsquo ; s year . Again this year , he was slated to do five sets at the Comedy Nest . All have been cancelled as well as another dozen coming gigs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve basically already lost about 30 per cent of my annual income and with no possibility of making anything until July . That is , if they&amp;rsquo ; re not cancelled , &amp;rdquo ; Seguin said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; On the bright side , I got lucky , because I was able to tape my last sets in Ottawa last weekend for an album , which should be out in a few months . But because of the situation now , I&amp;rsquo ; m thinking of calling the album , PanDerek. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Seguin , too , prefers not to take the gloom-and-doom approach . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve lived through terrible events like 9/11 and the Ice Storm , and they have brought out the best in people . I&amp;rsquo ; m trying not to be too self-involved , because there are people way worse off than me . I&amp;rsquo ; ve had a good run , making a good living by telling dick jokes for 12 years . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Seguin is welcoming the hiatus from work as time to get better acquainted with his three kids . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And it&amp;rsquo ; s taken just four days to realize that my kids don&amp;rsquo ; t like or respect me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;You 're Gon na Rire can be caught for free until Friday night on &lt;a href= " https : **29;8241;TOOLONG " &gt; ... NewsCoronavirus : Cirque du Soleil lays off more than half its staffhttp : **35;10460;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;10497;TOOLONG ... title= " Workers push poles into place to raise the Big Top at Jacques-Cartier Pier in preparation for the Cirque du Soleil 's Under the Same Sky in Montreal on Wednesday , March 4 , 2020. " alt= " Workers push poles into place to raise the Big Top at Jacques-Cartier Pier in preparation for the Cirque du Soleil 's Under the Same Sky in Montreal on Wednesday , March 4 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " John Mahoney " **28;10532;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1748382 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;10562;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Cirque du Soleil has laid off 2,600 people around the world following the cancellation of all of its shows because of concerns about the COVID-19 crisis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Late Monday afternoon , the Montreal-based circus &amp;mdash ; the world&amp;rsquo ; s largest live-touring company &amp;mdash ; confirmed that more than half of its global staff is being laid off indefinitely : 1,400 staff and technicians from its touring and resident shows and 1,200 artists from its shows . The company says all of the layoffs are temporary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cirque du Soleil employs 4,900 people around the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the weekend , the Cirque announced that it was suspending all of its touring and resident shows . That includes its six permanent shows in Las Vegas : O at Bellagio , K&amp;Agrave ; at MGM Grand , The Beatles Love at the Mirage , Myst&amp;egrave ; re at Treasure Island , Zumanity at New York-New York and Michael Jackson One at Mandalay Bay . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The shuttered touring shows include Under the Same Sky , which was supposed to have had its world premi&amp;egrave ; re at the Old Port in Montreal in April and then go on tour . Other touring big top shows shut down include Alegria in Austin , Chicago and Houston ; Bazzar in New Orleans and Salt Lake City ; Kooza in Tel Aviv ; Totem in Munich ; Volta in Costa Mesa and Denver ; and Kurios in Melbourne , Adelaide and Perth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It has also temporarily suspended its arena touring shows , notably Axel , which is touring the U.S. and Canada ; the Blue Man Group world tour in Switzerland , Netherlands and Portugal ; Corteo in France , Denmark , Sweden and Belgium ; Crystal in the U.K. and North America ; and Ovo in the U.S. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Almost none of the layoffs are at Cirque&amp;rsquo ; s head office in Montreal , which has 1,600 employees . About 100 people were laid off from the team working on Under the Same Sky in Montreal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Via email , the Cirque said : &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The company has shut down all its operations indefinitely until the coronavirus is controlled and its performers , employees and audience members are no longer at risk . We will continue to monitor and assess the situation to determine when shows will resume . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The Caisse de d ? p ? t et de placement du Qu ? bec recently increased its stake in the Cirque from 10 to 20 per cent when Cirque founder Guy Lalibert ? sold the remaining 10-per-cent stake that he had retained in the company . All touring staff are now not being paid whatsoever and are all essentially laid off indefinitely regardless of previously agreed-upon contract terms . NewsBrownstein : Margaret Trudeau takes audio route with her hit solo playhttp : **36;11713;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;11751;TOOLONG ... title= " " I 'm a mental-health advocate to the core , " Margaret Trudeau says . " alt= " " I 'm a mental-health advocate to the core , " Margaret Trudeau says . " data-portal-copyright= " Dave Sidaway " **28;11786;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1744795 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **33;11816;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was the surprise hit of last summer&amp;rsquo ; s Just for Laughs festival , but Margaret Trudeau&amp;rsquo ; s solo stage show , Certain Woman of An Age , could hardly be classified as Seinfeldian. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; True , Trudeau brought moments of great whimsy , but she also brought more moments of heart-wrenching emotion in three sold-out performances at the Ges&amp;uuml ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was a compelling and candid , warts and all , confessional in which Trudeau recounted her life-long battle with her demons . Nor did she hold back in recounting her dealings with a Who&amp;rsquo ; s Who of politicos and celebs &amp;mdash ; from Fidel Castro to Ted Kennedy , Mick Jagger to Jack Nicholson &amp;mdash ; she encountered along the way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; ll laugh . You&amp;rsquo ; ll cry , &amp;rdquo ; Trudeau had pledged . And , indeed , she left much of the audience doing both . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The show &amp;mdash ; written by Trudeau and Alix Sobler and directed by Kimberly Senior &amp;mdash ; also succeeded on another level : in helping to de-stigmatize mental illness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau gave only 14 performances of the play , here as well as in Toronto , Chicago and New York . But the good news is that the play was recorded live at New York&amp;rsquo ; s Minetta Lane Theatre and is now available as an audio presentation at &lt;a href= " https : //urldefense.com/v3/http : //audi ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;11851;TOOLONG ... title= " " I 'm a mental-health advocate to the core , " Margaret Trudeau says . " alt= " " I 'm a mental-health advocate to the core , " Margaret Trudeau says . " data-portal-copyright= " Dave Sidaway " **28;11886;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1744795 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau has been booked to do three performances of the play at Stratford in June and is also in discussions to take it on a Just for Laughs tour throughout Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m a mental-health advocate to the core , &amp;rdquo ; Trudeau said in a phone interview . &amp;ldquo ; I think everyone who has gone through a mental-health crisis , has sought treatment and has got well , has an obligation to share their hope with what they went through with others . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau is a survivor . Though animated and upbeat now , she went through hell for a long spell . She was committed three times to institutions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her life was hardly Camelot , when as a 23-year-old free spirit she married the 51-year-old , less-free-spirited Prime Minister Pierre Trudeau . She wasn&amp;rsquo ; t prepared for the protocol to come or life with Pierre . &amp;ldquo ; He was a feminist , as long as you weren&amp;rsquo ; t married to him , &amp;rdquo ; she cracks in the play . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Giving birth to her three sons , future prime minister Justin , Sacha and Michel , while in her mid-20s was to precipitate major post-partum depression issues . But it was the tragic death of her youngest son Michel in an avalanche in 1998 that so devastated her and just about did her in. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s one thing for an actor to play the painful life of a stranger , but quite another when it&amp;rsquo ; s the actor&amp;rsquo ; s own painful life being played . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Honestly , I had always wanted to be an actor , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; After my marriage to Pierre was over , I went down to New York to study . I really loved it . I was honing my skills , but then I found myself having to make a choice . I had to go back to Ottawa and raise my three little boys . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I ended up doing more reality TV in a sense , doing interviews . It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t supposed to be acting . But , then again , doing this play isn&amp;rsquo ; t really acting on a certain level , either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But actually to get to do this play &amp;hellip ; wow ! &amp;rdquo ; exclaims Trudeau , also the mother of Alicia and Kyle from her second marriage to her ex , Fred Kemper. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the real deal . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau gives full credit to director Senior and co-writer Sobler. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( Senior ) pushed me , and she pulled me where I needed to be . And ( Sobler ) was really able to help me have fun with the audience by getting them involved with the show rather than just me lecturing them about mental health . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re all hurting . Trying to have a happy and contributing life is the goal , but depression is the thief that takes you out . As a mania , it causes nothing but damage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Through humour we open another part of our brain . I like to be a bit comedic . Unfortunately my life has been a tragedy as well . I&amp;rsquo ; ve resolved a lot of pain and loss , and I&amp;rsquo ; ve learned that&amp;rsquo ; s life , too . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau was one of the women singled out in the &lt;a href= " https : **33;11916;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Montreal Gazette 's International Women 's Day editorial&lt;/a&gt; last Saturday as someone who has made a difference . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trudeau has found much support for her efforts in dealing with mental illness , both from the public and her kids . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Some of the stories may have been a little shocking to the kids , but they have all listened as I have listened to them . When one has a mental illness , all in the family are affected . So you have to work as a family together for healing , and we have . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While she lived under a microscope during her days with Pierre , she concedes that life for Justin and wife , Sophie Gr&amp;eacute ; goire , can&amp;rsquo ; t be a picnic with omnipresent social media she didn&amp;rsquo ; t deal with . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was isolated and cut off in my ivory tower , but Sophie is involved with so much in charities , in mental health . That makes all the difference . Life is difficult because of social media . But she&amp;rsquo ; s not just the wife . She has her own voice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Being just the wife of the prime minister is so archaic , &amp;rdquo ; said Trudeau , who is in self-isolation because she had recently travelled with Gr&amp;eacute ; goire , who has tested positive for COVID-19. &amp;ldquo ; I thought so at the time , &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And Trudeau paid the price for so thinking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But now I have a beautiful life for which I&amp;rsquo ; m most grateful . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Margaret Trudeau 's Certain Woman of an Age is available at &lt;a href= " https : //urldefense.com/v3/http : //audi ... $6.97 for members and $9.96 for non-members . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **32;11951;TOOLONG " **39;11985;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **28;12026;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;12056;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;12088;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;12130;TOOLONG ... It was the opening film of the Berlinale ; the coveted spot has proved beneficial &lt;a href= " https : **33;12518;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;despite early negative reviews&lt;/a&gt; from the industry trade publications and the Guardian . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;12553;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;12588;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **33;12624;TOOLONG ... title= " Philippe Falardeau , left , with TIFF co-director Cameron Bailey and CEO Piers Handling in 2011 , when Falardeau 's Monsieur Lazhar won the fest 's award for best Canadian feature . The opening slot positioned it as accessible and crowd-pleasing , so it seemed like a good combination . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; D&amp;eacute ; ry and McCraw have had great success on the festival circuit with movies including Denis Villeneuve&amp;rsquo ; s Incendies , which premi&amp;egrave ; red at the Venice Film Festival in 2010 ; and Falardeau&amp;rsquo ; s Monsieur Lazhar , which premi&amp;egrave ; red at the Locarno Film Festival in 2011 , where it won the audience award . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both movies went on to screen at the Toronto International Film Festival before landing Oscar nominations for best foreign-language film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( Premi&amp;egrave ; ring at a major European festival ) combined with going to Toronto after , which is where all the distributors are , becomes a very good way to launch a film internationally , &amp;rdquo ; D&amp;eacute ; ry said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For Canadian films , Toronto is super important and prestigious . " data-portal-copyright= " Pascal Le Segretain " **28;12973;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1742215 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Though C&amp;ocirc ; t&amp;eacute ; &amp;rsquo ; s bold filmmaking style has found a home at the Berlinale , it was discovered and championed at the Locarno Film Festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Locarno put me on the map , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I think my first six or seven films were Locarno premi&amp;egrave ; res . But a festival like Venice , I have never set foot in . I barely know them . They never answer my emails . I&amp;rsquo ; m not part of the Venice identity . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;13003;TOOLONG ... title= " " By getting into Sundance , already you 've got a mark of approval beside your film , " says Catherine Bainbridge , at the festival in 2017 with guitarist Stevie Salas , left , rapper Taboo and co-director Alfonso Maiorana for Rumble : The Indians Who Rocked the World . " alt= " " By getting into Sundance , already you 've got a mark of approval beside your film , " says Catherine Bainbridge , at the festival in 2017 with guitarist Stevie Salas , left , rapper Taboo and co-director Alfonso Maiorana for Rumble : The Indians Who Rocked the World . " data-portal-copyright= " Rezolution Pictures " **28;13038;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1742219 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Catherine Bainbridge and her Rezolution Pictures team were given the royal treatment when her film &lt;a href= " https : **33;13068;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Rumble : The Indians Who Rocked the World&lt;/a&gt; ( co-directed with Alfonso Maiorana ) had its world premi&amp;egrave ; re at the Sundance Film Festival in 2017 . It won the festival&amp;rsquo ; s World Cinema Documentary Special Jury Award for masterful storytelling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;13103;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;13139;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was huge , &amp;rdquo ; Bainbridge said , &amp;ldquo ; probably the biggest honour we&amp;rsquo ; ve had . We have had lots of great Canadian honours that are super important to us , but internationally Sundance was the one that brought us the most attention . By getting into Sundance , already you&amp;rsquo ; ve got a mark of approval beside your film . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bob Moore would have to agree . The producer at Montreal documentary company EyeSteelFilm attends 10 international film festivals on average per year , with Sundance being the holy grail . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The choice of where to premi&amp;egrave ; re your film &amp;mdash ; you don&amp;rsquo ; t always get a choice , &amp;rdquo ; he said , &amp;ldquo ; but there&amp;rsquo ; s definitely a strategy that goes into it . We&amp;rsquo ; re trying to give our films the best possible chance at a life outside festivals , so we try to think of festivals as a means , not an end . The reason you try to get into Sundance is so your film can get distributed as widely as possible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For us , the No. 1 film festival is Sundance . There are only 12 international docs in competition , so no matter what , you get the most possible exposure in the biggest market for docs right now in the world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Moore has brought films to Sundance seven times , beginning with Montrealer Yung Chang&amp;rsquo ; s Up the Yangtze in 2008 , and doubling up this year with Nairobi filmmaker Sam Soko&amp;rsquo ; s Softie and South African filmmaker Diana Neille&amp;rsquo ; s Influence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the Canadian market , it&amp;rsquo ; s all about Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s Hot Docs , he says . But after that , the playing field opens up . Moore recently returned from the True/False Film Festival in Columbia , Mo . He was accompanying Montrealer Mia Donovan and her film Dope Is Death , about Black Panthers and community activists using acupuncture to cure heroin addiction in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s and &amp;rsquo ; 80s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I guess we chose True/False for the premi&amp;egrave ; re because we felt like ( this film ) could benefit from the smaller , curatorial aspect of the lineup , &amp;rdquo ; Moore said . &amp;ldquo ; There are only a certain number of films , and the festival is growing in prominence . It felt like it made sense . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sundance is the only North American festival where it&amp;rsquo ; s a no-brainer for everyone to go there . For every other one , there&amp;rsquo ; s a special reason depending on the film . It&amp;rsquo ; s not a strict hierarchy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;13175;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;13206;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;13244;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;13271;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13304;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13346;TOOLONG ... we take Berlin : Quebec filmmakers swarm **30;13381;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13413;TOOLONG ... dawn for Canadian films at **29;13448;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13479;TOOLONG ... Indigenous filmmakers lead Quebec contingent at **25;13514;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; T'Cha Dunlevy , Montreal GazetteSat , 14 Mar 2020 15:29:33 **45;13541;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMontreal moviesNewsMontreal artists in limbo after coronavirus cancellationshttp : **27;13588;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;13617;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal electronic duo Pelada : Chris Vargas ( left ) and Tobias Rochman. " alt= " Montreal electronic duo Pelada : Chris Vargas ( left ) and Tobias Rochman. " data-portal-copyright= " Rebecca Storm " **28;13652;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1746314 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;13682;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was supposed to be a breakthrough spring tour in Europe and North America for Montreal-based underground electronic music duo Pelada . Instead , Tobias Rochman and Chris Vargas are stuck in a Brussels hotel , watching show after show get cancelled due to the COVID-19 outbreak . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Major music festivals such as Coachella in California and &lt;a href= " http : **34;13718;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;South by Southwest ( SXSW ) in Texas&lt;/a&gt; have been postponed or cancelled , depriving already financially precarious artists dependent on touring the ability to make ends meet . On Thursday , Montreal &lt;a href= " https : **33;13754;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;promoter Evenko suspended all shows with 250 or more people&lt;/a&gt; for the next 30 days based on measures put in place by the provincial government . The Orchestre symphonique de Montr&amp;eacute ; al ( OSM ) cancelled 11 concerts until April 5 , and their appearance with Kent Nagano at Carnegie Hall in New York March 24 will no longer happen . Popular Montreal indie band Wolf Parade scrapped its upcoming European tour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While major concert promoters and venues are feeling the effects , for many smaller local artists the inability to tour can be career-threatening. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; After six years as a group , this would have been our first profitable tour , &amp;rdquo ; Rochman said Thursday via Skype from Brussels , where they had unexpectedly camped the last three days . &amp;ldquo ; This was our first time working with a more professional agency out of the U.K. We were looking forward to this tour as the first major one after being signed and putting out our record . The shows we&amp;rsquo ; re playing are of a higher profile , with better pay . We&amp;rsquo ; ve been planning for this tour for more than a year . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pelada , who released the critically acclaimed Movimiento Para Cambio in October , were supposed to start the 15-date first leg of their European tour in Italy . That show was cancelled , as was their weekend show in Russia , which required they pay for a work visa upfront . They ended up in Brussels and were due to make their way Friday to Switzerland . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With their shows not going as planned and those expected guarantees &amp;mdash ; normally paid out after the show &amp;mdash ; gone , even an extra $140 for a hotel stay in Belgium can be a financial strain . Flights , trains and buses are other costs bands pay for in advance , and being able to exchange them for a later date isn&amp;rsquo ; t always a help . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Before we came , we applied for P-2 visa work permits for the United States because while we were gone , we needed it to process before our show April 17 at UCLA , &amp;rdquo ; said Vargas. &amp;ldquo ; That was around $2,000 we needed to make sure that was processed while we were here . And it&amp;rsquo ; s not even a guarantee we get the visa . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Added Rochman : &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; d be fine going home , but we don&amp;rsquo ; t even have the means to do that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band was set to play high-profile festivals like Primavera in Spain and Rewire in the Netherlands . With a touring itinerary set to last through the summer , Rochman and Vargas left their day jobs to pursue music full-time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you&amp;rsquo ; re living right on the threshold of profitability , it can be very dangerous , &amp;rdquo ; said Rochman. &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re in the gig economy , and the reality of seeing that bubble burst is devastating . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Micha&amp;euml ; l Bardier books and manages local and international artists with his Montreal company Heavy Trip . He estimates he&amp;rsquo ; s lost $250,000 in guarantees for his acts so far , in addition to flights and other costs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Honestly , the company has been in a good position lately . We&amp;rsquo ; ve been booking bigger tours in the U.S. and Europe , &amp;rdquo ; Bardier said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re not going to put the key in the door , but for sure , this is rough . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An added problem for Bardier and many others is bands route their tours around major North American festivals in the spring such as SXSW , Big Ears in Nashville and Mission Creek Festival in Iowa . All three have been cancelled . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Honestly , it&amp;rsquo ; s just really bad timing because last year around this time , I really determined March and April to be a really good period for international , experimental , world and jazz to tour in North America , so I really gave everything I had to route these tours , &amp;rdquo ; Bardier said . Right now everyone is scrambling to move their dates to the fall , when things will presumably be better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One artist set to play SXSW was Montreal rapper Naya Ali . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s actually been a long time dream of mine to attend , let alone perform at SXSW . It would have been a solid foot in door in the U.S. market , &amp;rdquo ; Ali said . &amp;ldquo ; However , I completely understand the seriousness of the situation and the measures that are being taken to protect all of our health . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13789;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13831;TOOLONG ... the latest in our coronavirus **29;13866;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Erik Leijon , Special to Montreal GazetteSat , 14 Mar 2020 10:00:19 **45;13897;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicNewsMontreal 's Kaie Kellough writes where identities meet , where genres collidehttp : **33;13944;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;13979;TOOLONG ... title= " Kaie Kellough 's Dominoes at the Crossroads is a set of 12 interwoven stories . The protagonists " almost all have ties to various West Indian backgrounds and places , " the Montreal author says , " but one of the things that grounds ( the book ) is that all of the main characters are Canadians . " " alt= " Kaie Kellough 's Dominoes at the Crossroads is a set of 12 interwoven stories . The protagonists " almost all have ties to various West Indian backgrounds and places , " the Montreal author says , " but one of the things that grounds ( the book ) is that all of the main characters are Canadians . " " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;14014;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1744809 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Kaie Kellough might have been a musician but for the fact that , like many of us , he found it harder than expected . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " I got acquainted with frustration , " the soft-spoken Montreal writer recalled of his early attempts at learning musical instruments . " It 's that realization that you ca n't just do something the way you want to do it , you have to follow a particular technique . You train yourself to the instrument , because the instrument wo n't adapt to you . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Stymied though he might have been by the violin , Kellough did n't give up on the idea of artistic expression . On moving to Montreal from Calgary in the late 1990s , the Vancouver-born son of mixed Guyanese parents soon found a supportive community in the local spoken-word scene -- where he did , in fact , find himself in numerous cross-disciplinary collaborations with musicians of various kinds . But it was in literature that he eventually found his true m ? tier . His 2016 debut novel Accord ? on , shortlisted for the Amazon Canada First Novel Award , was an experimental/satirical tour-de-force that announced the arrival of a major new Canadian voice ; last year 's Magnetic Equator was shortlisted for the QWF 's A.M. Klein Prize for Poetry . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Now , expanding Kellough 's remit further still , comes &lt;a href= " https : //kaie.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Dominoes at the Crossroads&lt;/a&gt; ( V ? hicule Press , 208 pages , $19.95 ) . An interwoven set of 12 stories occupying the fecund zone where memoir , fiction and essay meet , it 's a prismatic , lyrical , free-ranging , genre-fluid work that ends up functioning as a sort of cross-Canada travelogue of the Caribbean diaspora . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " They almost all have ties to various West Indian backgrounds and places -- Haiti , Guyana , St. Vincent , " Kellough said of his protagonists . " But one of the things that grounds ( the book ) is that all of the main characters are Canadians . The Canadian side of their identities is foregrounded ; their origins form a kind of ambient backdrop . It 's their experience working the intersection of Canadian culture and the identity they 've been brought up with that really interested me. " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;When it 's suggested that the book is marked by a deep sense of identification with his characters , Kellough readily concurs . " Sure . Having grown up with those ties , these are in a way portraits of people I 've always known , that I 've dated and had friendships with over the years . Writing about them is second nature to me. " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;14044;TOOLONG ... title= " Kaie Kellough says his mission statement is " to find a vocabulary for my experience " as a mixed-race Caribbean-Canadian. " alt= " Kaie Kellough says his mission statement is " to find a vocabulary for my experience " as a mixed-race Caribbean-Canadian. " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;14079;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1744790 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Music -- there 's that subject again -- is a major unifying thread in a book that , in less sure hands , might have spun off in a few directions too many . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " I wanted it to take place in different parts of Canada , and for there to be a constant sense of displacement , " Kellough said . " The setting of the story is never rooted and fixed , and I found the travelling musician a good metaphor for that . When you 're touring , you hit places in a superficial way -- you get a quick glimpse and a brief experience , and then you 're on to the next one . In a way it 's a fugitive existence , reflective of migrations and marooning , but also reflective of the progress of some kinds of improvised music . In a solo you 'll depart from the main melodic progression and eventually return to it . You 're never completely arriving . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;A Kellough story , while roughly incorporating the standard beginning , middle and end of narrative tradition , will often jump through holes in the time-space continuum . Historical figures like 18th-century firebrand Marie-Joseph Ang ? lique , who may or may not have deliberately burned down the colonial-era Old Port ( " It was an act of resistance that I 'd like to see commemorated , " said Kellough ) , and modern-day episodes like the &lt;a href= " https : **33;14109;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;1969 Sir George Williams occupation&lt;/a&gt; by Caribbean students ( " I do n't think it 's been properly historicized . Those events were remarkable , to say the least . They transformed people 's lives . They were part of a radical moment . " ) are illuminated through recontextualization . A minor Senegalese character in Hubert Aquin 's Next Episode is transposed to an underground hallway in Peel m ? tro station in a story inspired by a real-life African import shop . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " When someplace like that shop has been around for as long as it has , it becomes part of the imagination of the city , " Kellough said . " It gets embedded in the way we think of the place . " &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; **26;14144;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Investing otherwise overlooked figures with the dignity of literary attention is a Kellough hallmark ; another is his knack of shifting the perspective on a familiar subject in a way that shows it in a revelatory new light . One such case in the new book is Jack Kerouac 's On the Road , the generation-defining novel of Americans in search of their own country . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " In a book where so many people are on the road , I thought it would be fun to have that book make its cameo , " Kellough said . But there 's more to it than fun . The narrator of Petit Marronage , on reading Kerouac 's cult classic as an adult , is disappointed by the realization that its footloose white seekers " had free passage wherever they wanted . I wanted the narrator and Dean Moriarty to experience limitation , real life , to attempt to cross a border that they could not cross . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;A pertinent question to pose of a writer whose instincts have already led him to so many places and forms is that of driving principle . Does Kellough have an overarching mission statement ? &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " Yes , I do , " he said without hesitation . " It 's to find a vocabulary for my experience . When I was growing up in the ' 70s and ' 80s in Western Canada , there was the official rhetoric of multiculturalism that talked about how the state wanted to approach the existence in Canada of people like me and my parents . But it did n't say anything about what my experience , and the experience of other mixed-race Caribbean-Canadians , was actually like here . It was invisible . And when you 're invisible in the culture at large , one of the things you 're required to do is create your presence , and literature is my platform to do that . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " It 's not a question just of representation . It 's deeper than that . It 's a question of actual existence . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;&lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; **26;14172;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s2 " &gt;&lt;a href= " **29;14200;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **49;14231;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;14282;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;14324;TOOLONG ... age in the eye : Daniel J. It&amp;rsquo ; s happening at 4190 St. Denis St. on Saturday and Sunday from noon to 6 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; Montreal GazetteThu , 12 Mar 2020 15:00:03 **45;15430;TOOLONG ... review : Two very different revolutions at Segal Centre , TNMhttp : **37;15477;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;15516;TOOLONG ... title= " W.
