Island GazetteMystic Toolkit tackles esoteric elements in contemporary pop culturehttp : **33;24888;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;24923;TOOLONG ... title= " The Mystic Toolkit exhibit runs until March 8 at Stewart Hall Art Gallery , Pointe-Claire . Pictured is Jennifer Murphy 's work , The Shadow of Sirius. " alt= " The Mystic Toolkit exhibit runs until March 8 at Stewart Hall Art Gallery , Pointe-Claire . Pictured is Jennifer Murphy 's work , The Shadow of Sirius. " data-portal-copyright= " Alexis Bellavance " **28;24958;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728447 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The exhibition Mystic Toolkit , ongoing at Stewart Hall , is an artistic inquiry into what curator Ana&amp;iuml ; s Castro sees as an interesting trend among &lt;a href= " https : **33;24988;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the things I&amp;rsquo ; ve noticed is people in my generation are incredibly interested in alternative spiritual practices that were very popular in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s , and that are now resurging with a vengeance , &amp;rdquo ; said Castro . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mystic Toolkit includes game table installations by OMSK Social Club , a Berlin-based artist whose work lies at the intersection of art , LARP ( live action role-play ) , and RGP ( real game play ) ; as well as the intricate stitched works of intersecting circles by Isa Carrillo , a Mexican artist who is interested in the hidden and invisible phenomena inherent in everyday life . These works , along with others by Alex Coma , Hector Jimenez Castillo , Marigold Santos and Jennifer Murphy , speak to the theme of mystic connection central to the show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The qualities of contemplation and surrender , shared by mystics and the artists in this exhibition , are meant to be a reflection of an interest in the deeper invisible connections of existence . All of the artists are working at a time when climate change , spirituality rather than organized religion , and the challenges of hope in the face of rising political tensions , are bringing many of the issues from the &amp;rsquo ; 70s full circle , calling out for attention once more , Castro noted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jennifer Murphy&amp;rsquo ; s work &amp;lsquo ; The Shadow of Sirius&amp;rsquo ; lies in the centre of the gallery . On a raised circle covered with soil , a spiral of seashells swirls outward from the centre , echoing the spiral inherent in the form of each individual shell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What interested me in Jennifer&amp;rsquo ; s piece was how it captures the idea of rotation of the earth , rotation around the sun , and the solar system and the infinite rotation of the universe , &amp;rdquo ; said Castro . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Murphy said that her work is &amp;ldquo ; about ecology and loss&amp;rdquo ; , and much of her work comes from what she calls the &amp;ldquo ; assemblage&amp;rdquo ; of different things she collects. &amp;ldquo ; Being a collector is like being a hunter , and the chance and surprise of stumbling on something is part of what inspires me , &amp;rdquo ; she added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The shells in &amp;lsquo ; The Shadow of Sirius&amp;rsquo ; come from a collection of 1,500 shells from around the world that a woman in Ontario collected and logged throughout her life . When she died , her son put the collection in an online auction and Murphy bought it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As one stands looking at the spiral , or walks slowly around the work , one is witness to a collection that includes species that are now extinct . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I could tell by the logbook , that this was an elegy to what we&amp;rsquo ; re losing on the planet , &amp;rdquo ; said Murphy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alluding to the poetic form similar to a eulogy , Murphy invites viewers to contemplate the swirl of memory , time , and connection , and like the generation before them that pondered similar questions , this present generation of art makers contemplates what the future may hold for them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This work is also about loss , coupled with the fantastical , beauty and hope , &amp;rdquo ; said Murphy . &amp;ldquo ; Everyone is looking for a different way of being in the world , and asking different questions in different ways . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An art show conceived for people who remember the promise of an age of Aquarius , when &amp;ldquo ; peace will guide the planet and love will steer the stars&amp;rdquo ; , as well as for an audience of millennials , both will see their common concerns expressed in the art selected by Castro . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mystic Toolkit runs until March 8 at Stewart Hall Art Gallery , 176 Lakeshore Dr , Pointe-Claire. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;25023;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;25065;TOOLONG ... season of love -- eccentric , unmitigated , sour and soul -- is coming to **27;25100;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;25129;TOOLONG ... cradle linked two schools and four **32;25164;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;25198;TOOLONG ... ArtsWest Island GazetteDunlevy in Berlin : Ana ? s Barbeau-Lavalette 's rock ' n ' roll high schoolhttp : **34;25376;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;25412;TOOLONG ... title= " Quebec director Anais Barbeau-Lavalette in Montreal on Tuesday February 11 , 2020 . " alt= " Quebec director Anais Barbeau-Lavalette in Montreal on Tuesday February 11 , 2020 . Her new film La d ? esse des mouches ? feu premiered Saturday night , Feb. 22 , 2020 at the Berlin Film Festival . " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;25447;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1729333 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BERLIN &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; A shockwave rippled through the Generation 14plus section of the Berlinale &amp;mdash ; aka the &lt;a href= " https : **33;25477;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Berlin International Film Festival&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; on Saturday evening with the world premiere of Ana&amp;iuml ; s **27;25512;TOOLONG ; s La d&amp;eacute ; esse des mouches &amp;agrave ; feu ( Goddess of the Fireflies ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Quebec director&amp;rsquo ; s raucous adaptation of the novel of the same name by Genevi&amp;egrave ; ve Petterson is a sex- , drugs- and rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll-infused ride that begins on the 16th birthday of protagonist Catherine ( Kelley Depeault ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Barbeau-Lavalette pulls no punches in this sensorial account of the girl&amp;rsquo ; s turbulent , exhilarating passage from innocence to world-weary experience . Set in the cellphone- and internet-free bubble of the 1990s , it&amp;rsquo ; s a raw coming-of-age story the likes of which we rarely see on the big screen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film crashed through the sold-out Urania Berlin e.V . theatre like a tidal wave Saturday , sweeping up all in attendance and carrying them out to sea . Most impressive is that , as its name suggests , the festival&amp;rsquo ; s Generation 14plus section is geared to young film fans , 14 and up . There were viewers of all ages in attendance , including many teens , attending with friends or family . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was completely conscious , during the screening , of the adolescents discovering it , &amp;rdquo ; Barbeau-Lavalette said . &amp;ldquo ; It wasn&amp;rsquo ; t just adolescents , but I knew there were some , and to feel the reactions of these people at this turning point in their life &amp;mdash ; to hear them laugh , cry and reacting to the strong moments I had hoped for &amp;mdash ; it was touching . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The scariest thing for me was wondering whether the emotions would travel across borders , but there was something palpable in the room . Catherine&amp;rsquo ; s adolescence could easily be that of a young Berliner . From ( the moment I realized that ) , I knew the film could go anywhere . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;25541;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;25577;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carrying the movie is a fearless performance by Depeault , who at Saturday&amp;rsquo ; s premiere exhibited the same combination of spontaneity and charisma as her character , twirling giddily as the cast and crew made their entrance , just before the movie began . The 17-year-old actress had only a few credits to her name , but is sure to rise swiftly to prominence after this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; After the screening , she told me she felt like she just gave birth , &amp;rdquo ; Barbeau-Lavalette recounted. &amp;ldquo ; It was a trial by fire for her . In the film we see Catherine transform , but we also see an actress transform . It was an extraordinary feeling for her to finally present the film . She was really happy , and living a big moment . It was very emotional . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Barbeau-Lavalette has been to the Berlinale twice before , with Le Ring and &lt;a href= " https : **33;25613;TOOLONG ... , both films with socio-political subtexts . Here , she leaves behind the outside world and plunges into the visceral impact of a teenage girl throwing herself into life with abandon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to offer a frontal point-of-view on adolescence , &amp;rdquo ; Barbeau-Lavalette said . &amp;ldquo ; I had never seen a film that looked at this transformation , this age , the way I had lived it &amp;mdash ; as the height of the mutation of a character , without judgment , and embracing the vertigo of that passage , along with the pain and beauty it brings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This isn&amp;rsquo ; t a film that depends on a dramatic storyline . It&amp;rsquo ; s a story of transformation , so it had to be sensual ; you had to feel it in your gut . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; La d&amp;eacute ; esse des mouches &amp;agrave ; feu is an immersive cinematic achievement . Jonathan Decoste&amp;rsquo ; s intuitive cinematography , Sylvain Bellemare&amp;rsquo ; s impressionistic sound design , and St&amp;eacute ; phane Lafleur&amp;rsquo ; s dynamic editing round out a film that always feels perfectly in step with its wild young characters . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A triumphant soundtrack featuring everyone from David Bowie to Chuck Berry , The Cramps , Hole and Jon Spencer Blues Explosion , along with Quebec acts Offenbach and , tongue-in-cheek , Les B.B. , gives the whole thing an international flair reminiscent of Jean-Marc Vall&amp;eacute ; e&amp;rsquo ; s C.R.A.Z.Y . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It remains to be seen whether La d&amp;eacute ; esse des mouches &amp;agrave ; feu takes home any awards at the Berlinale . But this critic&amp;rsquo ; s fearless prediction says it&amp;rsquo ; s a definite contender , with a bright future ahead . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m very proud of the film , &amp;rdquo ; Barbeau-Lavalette said . &amp;ldquo ; You never know if people will absorb it with both their intellect and their senses . This is a movie that has to be felt . I wanted to make a sensitive film that is also rough . I was wondering if people would feel everything , but I think those things are universal . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;25648;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;25679;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;25717;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;25744;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;25771;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; T'Cha Dunlevy , Montreal GazetteMon , 24 Feb 2020 02:09:48 **45;25804;TOOLONG ... " " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;25922;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1727696 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;25952;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; During Rebecca Foon&amp;rsquo ; s first year in Montreal , back in 1996-97 , she would sometimes take a break from her studies at McGill to go play her cello at the corner of St-Laurent Blvd. and Prince Arthur St. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One day a homeless man stopped in his tracks and began to listen . He dropped to his knees , enrapt , where he remained for the next 15 minutes . He finally stood up , walked over to her open cello case and emptied his pockets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I felt so bad , but also so moved , &amp;rdquo ; Foon said , recalling that moment more than 20 years ago . &amp;ldquo ; I was like , &amp;lsquo ; Wow , music is powerful . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She never returned to perform on that corner , but she has gone on to create powerful moments , and movements , both on stage and in the global fight against climate change . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;25988;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;As co-founder of Pathway to Paris&lt;/a&gt; , a non-profit organization devoted to raising awareness about the urgency of climate action , she has rubbed shoulders with rock stars , activists , academics and politicians including Patti Smith , Thom Yorke , Naomi Klein , Michael Stipe , Cat Power , Flea and Talib Kweli , some of whom have become her friends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She is a former member of Montreal post-rock bands Thee Silver Mt . Zion Memorial Orchestra and Esmerine ( which won a Juno for best instrumental album in 2014 ) , and has put out two evocative &lt;a href= " https : **33;26023;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;solo projects under the name Saltland&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This week Foon released Waxing Moon , her first album under her own name . Although it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t address climate change directly , the song cycle expresses the overwhelming feeling of being a human facing extinction . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This album is very intimate for me , &amp;rdquo ; Foon said over tea on a snowed-in afternoon at her Rosemont home . &amp;ldquo ; I was exploring things I never had before , such as playing piano and singing &amp;mdash ; and the singing is much more raw and open . It just felt like the right moment to use my own name . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;26058;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;26093;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Though she describes this as her most song-based album , Foon doesn&amp;rsquo ; t make songs so much as soundscapes &amp;mdash ; stirring , emotionally loaded environments that envelop you , casting a spell that makes you forget what time it is , what day it is , where you are and what you were doing . That said , she approached this project with a sense of purpose . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I just can&amp;rsquo ; t make love songs right now , &amp;rdquo ; she said , while admitting this is partly a heartbreak album . &amp;ldquo ; I need to be talking and using music as a platform to talk about what&amp;rsquo ; s going on , and doing everything I can , with my limited capacity as a human , to fight for our planet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So this album is a more spiritual album . It&amp;rsquo ; s not as direct , lyrically , in terms of fighting climate change , but it&amp;rsquo ; s talking about that conversation , about love for our planet , love for each other . There&amp;rsquo ; s more to life than just this physical plane and reality . It&amp;rsquo ; s tapping into that . It&amp;rsquo ; s also just talking about a feeling of awakeness , and from that comes a hope for transformation . Because it&amp;rsquo ; s really easy to be numb , but the danger in being numb and apathetic is it leads to inaction . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Guests on the album include &lt;a href= " https : **33;26129;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Patrick Watson&lt;/a&gt; and his bassist Mishka Stein , Arcade Fire&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;26164;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Richard Reed Parry&lt;/a&gt; and Godspeed You ! Black Emperor violinist Sophie Trudeau . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;26199;TOOLONG ... title= " " As we saw with the Greta ( Thunberg ) protest , a quarter of the city has shown they care so much about climate change , " says Rebecca Foon . " It 's the critical issue of our time . " " alt= " " As we saw with the Greta ( Thunberg ) protest , a quarter of the city has shown they care so much about climate change , " says Rebecca Foon . " It 's the critical issue of our time . " " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;26234;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1727696 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26264;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;During POP Montreal in September&lt;/a&gt; , Foon brought her enhanced sensitivity to the mix as she and sax behemoth Colin Stetson joined legendary American avant-garde artist Laurie Anderson at the Rialto Theatre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She lives for such moments . They provide a balm &amp;mdash ; for herself and , she hopes , for others &amp;mdash ; against the enormity of the crisis at hand . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s actually why I make music , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; I care the most about connection and moving people . That&amp;rsquo ; s my version of spirituality . It&amp;rsquo ; s what I believe in , more than anything . I believe we come to earth as souls to awaken our hearts and inspire other hearts to awaken . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She has done just that with Pathway to Paris , which she launched with Jesse Paris Smith ( Patti Smith&amp;rsquo ; s daughter ) in 2014 , in the leadup to the Paris Agreement the following year . Emerging from a series of awareness-raising concerts , Pathway to Paris has since launched the 1000 Cities Initiative for Carbon Freedom , inviting cities to transition to 100 per cent renewable energy by 2040 . Montreal is not yet on that list , but Foon is hopeful . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think Montreal has an enormous opportunity right now to be a leading , flagship city of the world , because of our progressive mayor , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; And as we saw with &lt;a href= " https : **33;26299;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the Greta ( Thunberg ) protest&lt;/a&gt; , a quarter of the city has shown they care so much about climate change &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s the critical issue of our time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If hanging out with famous musicians seems a world away from the stark reality of climate change , not to mention from the experimental rock roots of the scene from which Foon emerged , it&amp;rsquo ; s all part of a continuum for her . She has been struck by the genuine sense of commitment from those offering to lend their name , ideas and artistry to the cause . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m just so moved by the compassion , and people truly caring about our planet and wanting to use their music as a platform for change , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; I find that deeply essential and inspiring right now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re in a state of emergency . All of us , if we care about the planet , need to be doing whatever we can to keep the planet alive &amp;mdash ; for our own dignity and karma . Whether you&amp;rsquo ; re a spiritual person or not , this is our time . It&amp;rsquo ; s not a joke anymore . We&amp;rsquo ; re losing Planet Earth , and an insane amount of species every day . If we care about our world and each other , and we want it to survive , how could we not ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " https : //www.rebeccafoon.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Waxing **29;26334;TOOLONG is out now . &lt;strong&gt;Rebecca Foon &lt;/strong&gt;performs April 29 at La Sala Rossa , 4848 St-Laurent Blvd. , with Brad Barr ; tickets cost $15 via &lt;a href= " http : **34;26365;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;26401;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicNewsTheatre : Harmony and hard times combine in The Times They Are A Changin'http : **32;26878;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;26912;TOOLONG ... title= " W. Joseph Matheson and Louise Pitre deliver a wealth of ' 60s protest music by songwriters of Jewish heritage in The Times They Are A Changin ' . Jewish songwriters of the era " had a gift for expressing the difference or otherness of people in general , " says Matheson . " Which is why their songs are so universal . " " alt= " W. Joseph Matheson and Louise Pitre deliver a wealth of ' 60s protest music by songwriters of Jewish heritage in The Times They Are A Changin ' . Jewish songwriters of the era " had a gift for expressing the difference or otherness of people in general , " says Matheson . " Which is why their songs are so universal . " " data-portal-copyright= " Joanna Akyol " **28;26947;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728176 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The sound of silence gives way to the sounds of the &amp;rsquo ; 60s at the Segal next week , as a musical show starring husband-and-wife team Louise Pitre and W. Joseph Matheson moves in just as the silent-retreat drama &lt;a href= " https : **33;26977;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Small Mouth Sounds&lt;/a&gt; moves out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Times They Are A Changin&amp;rsquo ; , which plays from March 1 to 22 , and which originated at Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s Harold Green Jewish Theatre Company in 2017 , is a celebration of protest songs from songwriters of Jewish heritage such as Carole King , Paul Simon , Mama Cass , Arlo Guthrie and , of course , Bob Dylan . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Segal audiences will still have searing memories of four-time Dora Award-winning Pitre&amp;rsquo ; s astonishing portrayal of &lt;a href= " https : **33;27012;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; ? dith Piaf in The Angel and the Sparrow&lt;/a&gt; a couple of years ago . That show also featured Matheson , as most of the men in Piaf&amp;rsquo ; s life . The two also performed together in Could You Wait ? , a musical written by Matheson based on the wartime romance between his parents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But The Times They Are A Changin&amp;rsquo ; offers more than a stage reunion featuring fondly remembered hits expertly sung . The songs have been carefully chosen to run the gamut from &amp;lsquo ; 60s idealism to disillusionment and anger . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Says Pitre , joining Matheson on a phone call with the Montreal Gazette : &amp;ldquo ; When the Harold Green theatre asked us to create the show , and when Joe and I got to researching , I think what surprised us the most was how heartbreaking it was that a lot of these songs could have been written today &amp;mdash ; that they still apply . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Matheson agrees that some of the content &amp;ldquo ; can be a bit more sombre than how people expect , &amp;rdquo ; but adds &amp;ldquo ; we still feel there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of hope&amp;rdquo ; in the show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In addition to a five-piece band , Pitre and Matheson will be accompanied by video imagery from Dan Bowman. &amp;ldquo ; We really felt we didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to just stand there singing songs , &amp;rdquo ; says Pitre. &amp;ldquo ; So there&amp;rsquo ; s a fair bit of video , which is a huge part of the show . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That became our accessory , &amp;rdquo ; says Matheson , &amp;ldquo ; in the sense that we could sing what everybody thinks is a lovely , hopeful song , but the video shows you the other side . ( You get ) what could have been and where we actually are . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Summing up a decade&amp;rsquo ; s worth of classic protest songs in 90 minutes naturally presented a logistical challenge for Pitre and Matheson . Inevitably , many favourites fell by the wayside . For Pitre , the most painful omission was Simon and Garfunkel&amp;rsquo ; s The Boxer . But the choices they made are by no means obvious ones . I confess I had to Google the three songs Pitre cites as the ones she feels most encapsulate the themes of the show ( One Tin Soldier , Last Night I Had the Strangest Dream and Save the Country ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As the show arrives at the Segal , one question that might be on an audience&amp;rsquo ; s mind is how many songs , if any , are we going to get from the rich catalogue of Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s favourite son . As it happens , there is a Leonard Cohen component , though perhaps not so much as the performers would have liked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Part of the issue , &amp;rdquo ; says Matheson , &amp;ldquo ; was that Avery Saltzman , one of the artistic directors of the Harold Green ( and the director of this show ) , was particularly determined not to go outside the lines . He&amp;rsquo ; d say , &amp;lsquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s an incredible song , but it&amp;rsquo ; s not really a protest song . &amp;rsquo ; I love Leonard Cohen , but unfortunately a lot of his songs didn&amp;rsquo ; t fit in . But we do reference him throughout the show as one of the voices of the &amp;rsquo ; 60s. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Keeping within the lines also meant that the songwriters had to be of Jewish heritage to qualify , though , as Pitre and Matheson point out , this in no way led to a narrowing of the field . Like the Golden Age of Broadway that came before , the &amp;rsquo ; 60s boasted an astonishing abundance of Jewish songwriters and singers , especially ones who raised their voices in protest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s an inherent sense of activism in that community , &amp;rdquo ; says Matheson . &amp;ldquo ; On the one hand they support one another , but they move that sense of activism in the community to the world at large . &amp;rdquo ; Jewish songwriters of the era , he adds , &amp;ldquo ; had a gift for expressing the difference or otherness of people in general . Which is why their songs are so universal . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **27;27047;TOOLONG Times They Are A Changin ' &lt;/strong&gt; is presented from March 1 to 22 at the Segal Centre , 5170 C ? te-Ste-Catherine Rd . Tickets : $67 ; student and under-30 discounts subject to availability . " data-portal-copyright= " Michele Tantussi " **28;28192;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728500 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BERLIN &amp;mdash ; The reviews are in , and they&amp;rsquo ; re not good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Philippe Falardeau&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;28222;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;My Salinger Year opened the 70th Berlinale&lt;/a&gt; &amp;mdash ; a.k.a. the Berlin International Film Festival &amp;mdash ; on Thursday , and no sooner had the film started than the industry trades and international media ( having viewed a press screening earlier in the day ) began posting their verdicts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28257;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Guardian called it " bafflingly insipid , zestless ( and ) derivative , " &lt;/a&gt; adding that it &amp;ldquo ; clunks hard enough to smash concrete . &amp;rdquo ; Well , tell us how you really feel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28292;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Variety disparaged Margaret Qualley 's turn as Joanna&lt;/a&gt; , an aspiring writer who moves to New York and finds work at the fabled literary agency Harold Ober Associates , while rightfully praising Sigourney Weaver&amp;rsquo ; s riotous performance as Margaret , Joanna&amp;rsquo ; s tough but ultimately warm-hearted boss . &lt;a href= " https : **33;28327;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Hollywood Reporter said the selection of My Salinger Year&lt;/a&gt; ( based on the 2014 memoir by Joanna Rakoff ) as the festival&amp;rsquo ; s opener suggests &amp;ldquo ; a taste for middlebrow fare more than innovation . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Falardeau was unhappy Friday afternoon , but for different reasons than you might think . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m disappointed , &amp;rdquo ; he said , &amp;ldquo ; and I&amp;rsquo ; m not talking about the reviews , which I haven&amp;rsquo ; t read and I&amp;rsquo ; m not going to . I&amp;rsquo ; m just disappointed I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t able to sit down and rediscover the film with my team and the audience . Something happened in Germany &amp;mdash ; something terrible . And rightfully so , the protocol changed last night and it became dramatic , and very deep , emotional and serious . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Politicians took the stage at the screening to speak out against &lt;a href= " https : **33;28362;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the shooting spree that left nine dead in the town of Hanau on &lt;/a&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28397;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Wednesday&lt;/a&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28432;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( They ) had to make speeches saying no to hate and violence , and I totally understand that , &amp;rdquo ; Falardeau said . &amp;ldquo ; Part of me was saying , &amp;lsquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t mind if the film is cancelled . I would totally accept that . &amp;rsquo ; But it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t cancelled , and the crowd is in this weird place when everything starts , and then I don&amp;rsquo ; t get to see the film . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the time Falardeau introduced his movie , over an hour after the preamble began , he was behind schedule to get to his next engagement &amp;mdash ; presenting the film to the general public at another theatre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He arrived there delighted to find real fans , who were excited to see the film and hear him speak . He returned to the premi&amp;egrave ; re screening only in time to see the final credits roll . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I got the feeling I had just missed the train , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He sat down next to Qualley and Weaver , who had been crying on Qualley&amp;rsquo ; s shoulder in the film&amp;rsquo ; s final stretch , and who turned to him and said , &amp;ldquo ; Where were you ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to experience that ( moment ) , &amp;rdquo ; Falardeau said , &amp;ldquo ; but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He took solace in Weaver&amp;rsquo ; s magnetic presence at the Berlinale , and her outspoken enthusiasm about the project . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She was here with her husband , and daughter , who never travels with her . She was like a kid yesterday . She stayed late at the party &amp;mdash ; she was one of the last to leave , talking to the crew and to everyone . That in itself is a huge gift for me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Weaver had only good things to say Friday about her reasons for making the film , her experience making it and the end result . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love ( my character&amp;rsquo ; s ) dry humour ; it&amp;rsquo ; s very cutting , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; She uses humour to keep people away . I don&amp;rsquo ; t get to play characters like that very much . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think the humour of the movie is delicious , the way the book geeks all talk to each other about everything . It&amp;rsquo ; s such a strange , ingrown world . I&amp;rsquo ; ve always loved comedy , more than anything . And the chance to be in a movie that is very meaningful but also very funny was important to me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Qualley was overwhelmed by the opportunity to work with one of her idols . They first met to run lines at Weaver&amp;rsquo ; s apartment on New York&amp;rsquo ; s Upper East Side , where , according to Falardeau , Qualley finally broke down and showered reverent praise on the veteran actress . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was just really excited to work with her , &amp;rdquo ; Qualley said . &amp;ldquo ; She&amp;rsquo ; s such an icon . Alien &amp;mdash ; come on ! She&amp;rsquo ; s so graceful . One of the many things I admire about her is her ability to have this authoritative presence , and at the same time this youthful innocence and joy to her . It&amp;rsquo ; s a really awesome combination . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The relationship between the women , on and off screen , is something Falardeau believes can only help My Salinger Year . While it has been sold in many countries , the film is still looking for a U.S. distributor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I feel we should concentrate on getting ( the film ) to the right audience , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve been receiving emails from women I don&amp;rsquo ; t know , and messages from women on Facebook . The film is inspiring to them . The distributors have to focus on that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;28467;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;28498;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;28536;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;28563;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;28596;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28638;TOOLONG ... in Berlin : Philippe Falardeau squints into Berlinale **30;28673;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28705;TOOLONG ... we take Berlin : Quebec filmmakers swarm **30;28740;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28772;TOOLONG ... from T'Cha **28;28807;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; T'Cha Dunlevy , Montreal GazetteFri , 21 Feb 2020 19:36:21 **45;28837;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMontreal moviesNewsTen years after her death , Lhasa de Sela 's legacy matters as much as everhttp : **36;28884;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;28922;TOOLONG ... title= " Lhasa de Sela at the Montreal Spectrum in 2004 . " What I remember most is the feeling that there was no separation between performer and audience , " her brother Mischa Karam recalls of seeing her on stage , " that everyone in the room was having this collective experience in the moment . " " alt= " Lhasa de Sela at the Montreal Spectrum in 2004 . " What I remember most is the feeling that there was no separation between performer and audience , " her brother Mischa Karam recalls of seeing her on stage , " that everyone in the room was having this collective experience in the moment . " " data-portal-copyright= " PHIL CARPENTER " **28;28957;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728114 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You don&amp;rsquo ; t meet many people who &amp;lsquo ; kind of like&amp;rsquo ; Lhasa , &amp;rdquo ; said Mischa Karam. &amp;ldquo ; If you know about her at all , you probably love her . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lhasa de Sela had that effect on people &amp;mdash ; and through her work , she still does . The multilingual singer and songwriter , who died 10 years ago last month at age 37 , left three albums of remarkable depth and breadth &amp;mdash ; Latin American and European musical forms brought together in a singular vision , a lyrical style at once plain-spoken , poetic and mystical , all informed with a deep sense of melancholy . An adopted Montrealer , she came to be synonymous with the city that embraced her . Her presence is still felt here ; her absence still hurts . But her spirit abides. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Karam speaks from a special perspective &amp;mdash ; he is , after all , de Sela&amp;rsquo ; s brother , and now officially oversees her legacy . And as he is in a good position to know , Lhasa continues to make new friends . One of those is American writer Fred Goodman , best known for his 1997 book The Mansion on the Hill , a definitive study of the backroom managerial machinations behind some of rock&amp;rsquo ; s most iconic artists . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I heard Lhasa&amp;rsquo ; s music for the first time the week she died , &amp;rdquo ; Goodman recalled by email from his home in New York . &amp;ldquo ; I knew she was the real deal and that I had to know more about her . As I dug into her life and music , I began to feel that other Americans should be encouraged to discover her work and story . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The fruit of Goodman&amp;rsquo ; s missionary zeal is &lt;a href= " https : **33;28987;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Why Lhasa de Sela Matters&lt;/a&gt; ( University of Texas Press , 181 pages , $25.50 ) . Published in November , it&amp;rsquo ; s a slim but illuminating book that fills a gap and serves a dual purpose : the ideal introduction for Lhasa neophytes , it also offers fans entry into deeper currents of her music and sensibility . In the process , it amply justifies its title . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Her tastes and values were formed while living in a very specific bubble &amp;mdash ; one that was politically , socially and spiritually charged , &amp;rdquo ; said Goodman . &amp;ldquo ; The Kinks have a wonderful song , I&amp;rsquo ; m Not Like Everybody Else , and in their case it was a statement of aspiration . In Lhasa&amp;rsquo ; s case , it was a fact . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;29022;TOOLONG ... title= " " Lhasa had a vision of the world -- a romantic and hopeful vision that life is not simply tragic , but magic , " says biographer Fred Goodman , whose book Why Lhasa de Sela Matters was published in November . " alt= " " Lhasa had a vision of the world -- a romantic and hopeful vision that life is not simply tragic , but magic , " says biographer Fred Goodman , whose book Why Lhasa de Sela Matters was published in November . " data-portal-copyright= " Audiogram " **28;29057;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728138 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That uniqueness was nurtured in a peripatetic childhood that reflected the philosophy of her parents . Educator Alejandro Sela and photographer Alexandra Karam held to the ideals of the &amp;rsquo ; 60s counterculture long past the point where many gave them up . Ranging all over the United States and Alejandro&amp;rsquo ; s native Mexico in a family bus &amp;mdash ; Lhasa was born in an alternative community near Woodstock , N.Y . in 1972 &amp;mdash ; they allowed their four daughters the freedom to follow their interests . Conventional structured education was sporadic ; life education was constant . The kids were presented with a multicultural , progressive range of listening and reading options . There was no TV . Even their third daughter&amp;rsquo ; s name reflects a decidedly non-mainstream outlook . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I found ( the name ) , &amp;rdquo ; Alexandra recalled last week in the Petite-Patrie apartment where she has lived since settling in Montreal full time three years ago . &amp;ldquo ; I was reading a lot about Tibet and Buddhism at the time . I looked at this little girl&amp;rsquo ; s face , and it just felt right : Lhasa . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Was there a time when she first sensed something special in Lhasa ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;29087;TOOLONG ... title= " Lhasa , left , Ayin and Sky de Sela in Tivoli , N.Y . in 1977 . Their sister Miriam was being born in the room behind them . " alt= " Lhasa , left , Ayin and Sky de Sela in Tivoli , N.Y . in 1977 . Their sister Miriam was being born in the room behind them . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of Alexandra Karam " **28;29122;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728113 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; With all those four daughters ( Mischa is her son from a subsequent marriage ) I had such absolute faith in all that I saw was within them , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; It never surprised me where they took their gifts , because they developed them so early . Lhasa was writing poems when she was five . I&amp;rsquo ; m often very critical , but Lhasa was very , very good . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A good indication of de Sela&amp;rsquo ; s precociousness , and of cultural interests hardly typical of an American child of the 1970s , was her determination at an early age that she would one day marry Victor Jara , not knowing for several years that the Chilean folksinger died a year after she was born , killed for his open defiance of the Pinochet regime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; De Sela&amp;rsquo ; s three sisters all became circus performers . It was their training at the &amp;Eacute ; cole nationale de cirque that drew her to Montreal in 1991 , wanting to be closer to them &amp;mdash ; a move that , with hindsight , takes on the feel of destiny . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was here that she took her first tentative steps into live performance and found a crucial &lt;a href= " https : **33;29152;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;musical soulmate and mentor in guitarist Yves Desrosiers&lt;/a&gt; ; their collaboration would lead to her 1997 debut , La Llorona . A Spanish-sung album that has achieved the status of an unquestioned classic , it was also a commercial success , going platinum in Canada and selling over half a million copies worldwide . In France it made her a bona fide star . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Twelve years Lhasa&amp;rsquo ; s junior , Mischa Karam saw his half-sister only intermittently until moving to Montreal himself in 2005 . Seeing her in front of an audience for the first time was a revelation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What I remember most , &amp;rdquo ; he recalled , &amp;ldquo ; is the feeling that there was no separation between performer and audience , that everyone in the room was having this collective experience in the moment . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Having achieved a debut splash most artists could only dream of , de Sela proceeded to flout every rule of conventional career-building by joining her sisters&amp;rsquo ; one-ring travelling circus in Europe . &amp;ldquo ; She had the strength to not listen to anyone&amp;rsquo ; s advice , to go her own way , &amp;rdquo ; her mother said . &amp;ldquo ; That takes courage . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;29187;TOOLONG ... title= " Lhasa de Sela in 1998 . " There were so many different things she wanted to do , but I ca n't imagine her ever not singing , " says mother Alexandra Karam. " alt= " Lhasa de Sela in 1998 . " There were so many different things she wanted to do , but I ca n't imagine her ever not singing , " says mother Alexandra Karam. " data-portal-copyright= " RICHARD ARLESS JR . " **28;29222;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728116 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Far from hurting her progress , this year-long interregnum served to burnish her legend : plenty of people talk figuratively of running away to join the circus , but de Sela literally did it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After folding up the circus tent , de Sela chose for a time to live in Marseille . She made her way back to Montreal by early 2003 , having abandoned sessions in France for what was to become her second and arguably best album , the Spanish/French/English collection The Living Road . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The return to the city where she had found her musical calling &amp;mdash ; a welcoming place where few thought it odd for an American-born English-speaking artist to be singing in Spanish and French &amp;mdash ; cemented Montrealers&amp;rsquo ; tendency to claim de Sela as their own . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Montreal does claim her , but that&amp;rsquo ; s fair &amp;mdash ; she claimed Montreal , too , &amp;rdquo ; said Mischa Karam. &amp;ldquo ; That first record&amp;rsquo ; s success started here , remember . And who knows &amp;mdash ; maybe it wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have been the same in another place , where she might have been pigeonholed as &amp;lsquo ; world music&amp;rsquo ; or some other thing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She would have been an artist wherever she was &amp;mdash ; but a different one , &amp;rdquo ; Goodman opined. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; De Sela&amp;rsquo ; s next major artistic decision &amp;mdash ; the choice to write and record a set of songs in English &amp;mdash ; may have been at least partly down to a desire to be heard more widely in the country of her birth . The U.S. had lagged far behind Quebec and Europe in embracing her work , with sales of her first two albums there representing only a tiny percentage of their total . But that certainly wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the only reason . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; English was , after all , her first language , &amp;rdquo ; said Mischa Karam. &amp;ldquo ; She had put down roots in an English-speaking neighbourhood . She was listening to people like Al Green , Cat Power , Bonnie ( Prince ) Billy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;29252;TOOLONG ... title= " " Montreal does claim ( Lhasa de Sela ) , but that 's fair -- she claimed Montreal , too , " says brother Mischa Karam , with mother Alexandra Karam in 2015. " alt= " " Montreal does claim ( Lhasa de Sela ) , but that 's fair -- she claimed Montreal , too , " says brother Mischa Karam , with mother Alexandra Karam in 2015. " data-portal-copyright= " Stephane Bingham " **28;29287;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728117 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She wanted to be heard in America and had already seen how resistant the country and its gatekeepers in the media were to her French and Spanish songs , &amp;rdquo ; Goodman said . &amp;ldquo ; She made that record ( 2009&amp;rsquo ; s eponymous Lhasa ) with America in mind . And it&amp;rsquo ; s not something that kowtows to American tastes . Quite the opposite . It&amp;rsquo ; s extremely personal and defies commercial expectations . She simply offered American listeners a version of herself that they could understand . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was at this time , seemingly the most happy and settled in her life &amp;mdash ; she bought a house on Esplanade Ave . in Mile End and embarked on a relationship with local mastering engineer Ryan Morey &amp;mdash ; that de Sela got sick . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The breast cancer that was to claim her life went undetected in an early misdiagnosis , causing a crucial near-two years of possible treatment to be lost . By the time the cancer was identified , said her mother , &amp;ldquo ; it was too late . &amp;rdquo ; Lhasa de Sela died on New Year&amp;rsquo ; s Day , 2010 . Many Montrealers still recall that her passing was followed by four straight days of heavy snow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While it&amp;rsquo ; s impossible to know what de Sela would be doing were she still alive , educated guesses can be made . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think she wanted to make more music , and at a quicker pace than what she had been used to , &amp;rdquo ; Mischa Karam said . &amp;ldquo ; The seven years between albums , with a circus or an existential crisis or maybe both in between , that sense of moulting and regenerating &amp;hellip ; I think she wanted less of that . She talked about building a studio and rehearsal space of her own , improvising more , collaborating more . She mentioned people like Godspeed You ! Black Emperor . She was planning an album of covers of Victor Jara and Violeta Parra songs . She was writing a book . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the really sad things about the timing of her death is that she was ready to open herself to the community where she was putting down roots , and go into things with a little less control , just to see what happened . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There were so many different things she wanted to do , but I can&amp;rsquo ; t imagine her ever not singing , &amp;rdquo ; said Alexandra Karam. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thoughts of what might have been are channelled by Goodman in an unexpected touch . He ends his written account of de Sela&amp;rsquo ; s life and work with The Girl-Fish , a brief Catalonian folk tale on the theme of regeneration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Lhasa had a vision of the world &amp;mdash ; a romantic and hopeful vision that life is not simply tragic , but magic , &amp;rdquo ; said Goodman . &amp;ldquo ; I added the folk tale as a postscript in the belief that she would prefer her romantic and mystical vision of life to stand as the last word . As Lhasa said in one of her songs , &amp;lsquo ; There is no end to this story . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regeneration and its implicit faith in the future brings us back to Alexandra Karam&amp;rsquo ; s belief in her children&amp;rsquo ; s gifts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In fact , I feel that way about children , period , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; If they&amp;rsquo ; re given the opportunity and the space to develop what really interests them , what they love , they&amp;rsquo ; re going to do amazing things . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In that light , it&amp;rsquo ; s all the more poignant to consider Parc Lhasa-De Sela , the little urban oasis at the northern edge of Mile End that was officially dedicated in her name in 2014. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s my favourite park in the neighbourhood for young kids , &amp;rdquo ; said Montreal writer Saleema Nawaz , mother of a five-year-old . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s totally self-contained and it has everything you want : play structures , swings , a sandbox , green space , a hill , picnic tables , a splash pad . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some of those children are bound to be curious , and to ask their parents about this Lhasa person whose name this welcoming space bears . And the legend will continue to grow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;29317;TOOLONG ... title= " Lhasa de Sela and mother Alexandra Karam in 2000 . " She had the strength to not listen to anyone 's advice , to go her own way , " Karam says . " That takes courage . " " alt= " Lhasa de Sela and mother Alexandra Karam in 2000 . " She had the strength to not listen to anyone 's advice , to go her own way , " Karam says . " That takes courage . " " data-portal-copyright= " Richard Arless Jr . " **28;29352;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1728115 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Five roads to Lhasa&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lhasa de Sela left a small but near-flawless body of work . All three of her studio albums &amp;mdash ; 1997&amp;rsquo ; s La Llorona , 2003&amp;rsquo ; s The Living Road and 2009&amp;rsquo ; s Lhasa &amp;mdash ; are recommended unreservedly , so picking a highlight from each is an exercise in pure subjectivity . Still , here are three suggestions , along with a pair to represent two areas in which her art also thrived : collaborations and live performances . &lt;/p&gt; **31;29382;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The alchemy achieved by de Sela and Yves Desrosiers on her career-making debut album is nowhere better demonstrated than on this rhythmically eccentric song co-written with her father , its portmanteau title meaning the flower ( flor ) and the song ( canto ) . The stop-and-start arrangement complements a vocal of remarkable empathy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **33;29415;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29450;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;La Frontera&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s an irony illustrative of de Sela&amp;rsquo ; s gifts as an enabling musical synthesist that one of the most Mexican songs in her catalogue should feature as its lead instrument the trumpet of Lebanese-French musician Ibrahim Maalouf , whose Middle Eastern stylings fit into the ranchera arrangement perfectly . Meanwhile , de Sela demonstrates a poet&amp;rsquo ; s knack for blending the metaphysical and the prosaic . At a time when certain world leaders are putting up new barriers , a song like this is invaluable . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;29486;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29522;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;That Leaving Feeling&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; De Sela&amp;rsquo ; s friendship with Stuart A. Staples , leader of the cult English band Tindersticks , led to three collaborations : two with the group proper and this gem from 2006 , when she joined him on his solo album Leaving Songs . An exquisite duet on a song whose theme of restlessness and romantic misgiving could have been tailor-made for her . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;29558;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29594;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;Who by Fire&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many Leonard Cohen songs have been covered by many , many people . But you&amp;rsquo ; d be hard pressed to find a better case of a fellow artist inhabiting his work than de Sela&amp;rsquo ; s performance of this song , done as part of a 2008 Cohen tribute night at the Montreal International Jazz Festival and watchable on YouTube. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; See also : &lt;a href= " https : **33;29630;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Live in Reykjavik&lt;/a&gt; . Recorded in 2009 , when de Sela was weakened by her illness but determined to perform what would be her last songs , it&amp;rsquo ; s invaluable as the only official full-length account of her ability to command a stage , and of her uncanny interplay with her band . &amp;ldquo ; They were all thinking together , always , &amp;rdquo ; said Mischa Karam. &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s so much listening going on among them . The way Lhasa put it was , &amp;lsquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re all singing , but I have the mouth . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;29665;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;29701;TOOLONG &lt;h3&gt;I 'm Going In&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chosen by both her brother and her mother as their favourite Lhasa song , this stately piece from the final album was , said Mischa Karam , &amp;ldquo ; the first song that she could accompany herself on ( on the piano ) . She was very proud of that &amp;mdash ; it was something she really worked hard for . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With its opening of &amp;ldquo ; When my lifetime had just ended / and my death had just begun , &amp;rdquo ; it&amp;rsquo ; s a song that has been prone to misinterpretation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everyone assumes that it&amp;rsquo ; s a song about death , &amp;rdquo ; said Alexandra Karam. &amp;ldquo ; But it isn&amp;rsquo ; t . It&amp;rsquo ; s a song about deciding to go into life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s2 " &gt;&lt;a href= " **29;29737;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **49;29768;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;29819;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;29861;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicDunlevy in Berlin : Philippe Falardeau squints into film fest spotlighthttp : **31;30111;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;30144;TOOLONG ... title= " Director Philippe Falardeau and actress Margaret Qualley attend the opening gala of the 70th Berlinale International Film Festival in Berlin on Thursday . " alt= " Director Philippe Falardeau and actress Margaret Qualley attend the opening gala of the 70th Berlinale International Film Festival in Berlin on Thursday . " data-portal-copyright= " ANNEGRET HILSE " **28;30179;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1727771 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BERLIN &amp;mdash ; Philippe Falardeau was still processing the scale of what he was living , Thursday afternoon . The Quebec director&amp;rsquo ; s latest film My Salinger Year was about to officially launch the 70th Berlinale &amp;mdash ; a.k.a. the Berlin International Film Festival &amp;mdash ; on Thursday evening . And a lot of fuss was being made . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a big spotlight for a small film , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s how I feel . But I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t refuse this moment . Part of me wants it to be over , and part of me is saying : &amp;lsquo ; Slow down , because this won&amp;rsquo ; t happen again . You have to understand what&amp;rsquo ; s happening and experience it , accompany your actors and not wish that the brouhaha ends here . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And brouhaha there was . At the jam-packed press conference after the mid-day press screening of the film , Sigourney Weaver received the royal treatment , and became the focus of most of the questions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Weaver is not the star of the film &amp;mdash ; or at least she&amp;rsquo ; s not supposed to be . But star power is impossible to repress . Her supporting turn as a veteran tough-nosed New York literary agent is delightfully scene-stealing. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But rising star Margaret Qualley holds her own portraying Joanna Rakoff , the real-life writer on whose memoir the film is based . Rakoff spent a year working as a secretary for J.D. Salinger&amp;rsquo ; s literary agent in the &amp;rsquo ; 90s . Qualley brings quirk and charisma to the part of a young woman searching for her voice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Because despite the enticing title , this movie isn&amp;rsquo ; t really about Salinger at all . He appears intermittently at the other end of the phone line , which Joanna answers with friendly enthusiasm , if not awe &amp;mdash ; she had never read anything by Salinger when she got the job . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nor had Falardeau when he began making his movie . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Salinger for me , was a really relevant writer that I hadn&amp;rsquo ; t read , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; ( Joanna ) takes a while to read Salinger , so I put myself in her shoes . I knew of Salinger , his importance and aura . But I wrote my first draft ( of the script ) without reading him . Then when I read him , I had an epiphany , as Joanna did in real life &amp;mdash ; but I did it at 49. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Most people read him when they&amp;rsquo ; re young , in their adolescence . I didn&amp;rsquo ; t expect to like it , but I liked it very much , probably more than I would have in my teens . It was a revelation . After that , I made sure this was not a film about Salinger , but about the effect of a writer on a young woman , and her journey to becoming a writer . He&amp;rsquo ; s more like a phantom presence , hovering over her . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;30209;TOOLONG ... title= " Actors Hamza Haq , Douglas Booth , actress Margaret Qualley , director Philippe Falardeau , actress Sigourney Weaver , actor Yanic Truesdale , actress Xiao Sun , author , screenwriter and executive producer Joanna Rakoff , and an unidentified man walk the red carpet in Berlin on Thursday . " alt= " Actors Hamza Haq , Douglas Booth , actress Margaret Qualley , director Philippe Falardeau , actress Sigourney Weaver , actor Yanic Truesdale , actress Xiao Sun , author , screenwriter and executive producer Joanna Rakoff , and an unidentified man walk the red carpet in Berlin on Thursday . " data-portal-copyright= " MICHELE TANTUSSI " **28;30244;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1727921 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; My Salinger Year had its world premiere Thursday night at the grandiose Berlinale Palast theatre , where Weaver , Qualley and stars such as Jeremy Irons &amp;mdash ; the head of this year&amp;rsquo ; s jury &amp;mdash ; and others posed for fans and shouting paparazzi . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not easy to show up in front of a wall of cameras , with people screaming , &amp;rdquo ; Falardeau said following the afternoon press conference , still trying to reconcile the film he had made with the platform it had been given . &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to keep it in the family , but it&amp;rsquo ; s not happening in the family . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can say , &amp;lsquo ; Okay , it&amp;rsquo ; s a film that is honest . &amp;rsquo ; But to offer it this world stage , you start to ask yourself questions . Is it over-the-top in a way that isn&amp;rsquo ; t good for the film ? Maybe . It makes your head spin , but it&amp;rsquo ; s out of your control . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the other major supporting characters of the film is New York City . Or rather , Montreal as New York City . ( One Big Apple reporter at the press conference actually thanked Falardeau for showing New York as New York , to his great excitement . ) A striking confluence of evocative art direction , playful costume design and the bold cinematography of Sara Mishara converge to make a visually mesmerizing film that brings a twilight touch to the last , early-internet days of literature&amp;rsquo ; s reign . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Falardeau , all of these elements work together to create a movie that moves at its own slow , steady , earnest pace . It&amp;rsquo ; s a funny film , too , as Falardeau films often are &amp;mdash ; but not as funny as it could have been . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There was a lot more humour worked into each scene , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; But what I wanted people to retain is something a bit more nuanced , subtle and poetic , so I made the decisions I thought I had to take for the film I wanted to make . Maybe it works , maybe it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t . But it&amp;rsquo ; s an honest film , from the heart . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;30274;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;30305;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;30343;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;30370;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30403;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;30445;TOOLONG ... we take Berlin : Quebec directors swarm **30;30480;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;30512;TOOLONG ... brain loves music : Montreal doc shows healing power of **25;30547;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;30574;TOOLONG ... do so many Quebec films make it to the Oscar live-action short category ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; T'Cha Dunlevy , Montreal GazetteFri , 21 Feb 2020 00:56:40 **45;30609;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMontreal moviesNewsA season of love -- eccentric , unmitigated , sour and soul -- is coming to Hudsonhttp : **34;30656;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;30692;TOOLONG ... title= " Leni Parker plays seven characters in the one-woman play The Blonde , The Brunette and the Vengeful Redhead , at Hudson Village Theatre , beginning April 16. " alt= " Leni Parker plays seven characters in the one-woman play The Blonde , The Brunette and the Vengeful Redhead , at Hudson Village Theatre , beginning April 16. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;30727;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1724275 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Expressions of love , be it revenge in the wake of love gone sour , love in a world of eccentricity , singles in search of soulmates or the unmitigated love of a child for his mother , are coming to the Hudson Village Theatre , beginning in April . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each of the four plays in Hudson Village Theatre&amp;rsquo ; s 2020 professional season looks at love through a distinct lens . Single tickets and multi-play passes are on sale now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Artistic director Dean Patrick Fleming has expanded the season by one production this year , allowing him to select plays to launch and close the series that reach beyond the pure fun of conventional summer stock theatre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First up is Leni Parker in The Blonde , The Brunette and the Vengeful Redhead ( April 16-26 ) . Parker was in the HVT production of Marion Bridge last summer . Fleming directs the one-woman , murder-mystery-comedy by Australian playwright Robert Hewett. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This one looks at what happens when you love a bit too hard , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Parker plays seven characters through eight monologues , ranging from a preschool boy to a 45-year-old man to a 90-year-old woman . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The story unravels , bit by bit , as we meet the people who were in the ( murdered person&amp;rsquo ; s ) life , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The season closes with another solo show , Duncan Macmillan&amp;rsquo ; s Every Brilliant Thing ( Oct. 22-Nov. 1 ) . Daniel Brochu stars and Fleming directs . Brochu was in the HVT production of Art in 2018. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brochu plays a fellow who , as a seven-year-old , creates a list of things to love about life to give to his mother , who suffers from depression and has attempted suicide . As he grows older , the list grows longer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s one of the best things I&amp;rsquo ; ve ever read , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s scary , but fantastic . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The play unfolds within a reconfigured performance space , with audience members sitting onstage as well as front of house . This accommodates interaction between Brochu and members of the audience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;A season book-ended by two solo productions with demanding narratives. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you are dealing with one actor , the level of trust has to be huge , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s why I chose Leni and Daniel . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Multi-award-winning theatre director Micheline Chevrier comes to Hudson to direct Pulitzer Prize-winning American playwright John Patrick Shanley&amp;rsquo ; s Outside Mullingar , starring Brian Dooley , Pauline Little and Kathleen Stavert ( July 3-19 ) . Dooley was in the HVT production of The Drawer Boy in 2019. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Outside Mullingar tells the tale of an introverted farmer and his spinster neighbour who yearns to capture not only his attention , but his love . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a quirky , romantic notion of what rural Ireland is like , written by a New Yorker , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &amp;ldquo ; Shanley has always been one of my favourite playwrights. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shanley won the Pulitzer Prize for Drama for Doubt : A Parable and an Oscar for Best Original Screenplay for the movie Moonstruck. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Strawberries in January is by Governor General Award-winning playwright &amp;Eacute ; velyne de la Cheneli&amp;egrave ; re and translated by GG-winner Morwyn Brebner ( Aug. 6-23 ) . It traces the stories of four Montreal singles in search of love . Fleming directs Quincy Armorer , Lucinda Davis , Mike Payette and Warona Stshwaelo . Fleming said the play stands apart because of its almost cinematic structure . Told through a series of flashbacks , he said it&amp;rsquo ; s as if &amp;ldquo ; two plays are happening at the same time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;This season will be the first for Fleming as a bonafide Hudsonite . He sold his home in Notre-Dame-de-Gr ? ce and will have settled into his Hudson home by the time the season begins . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve been talking about moving out of the city for years , &amp;rdquo ; Fleming said . &amp;nbsp ; &amp;ldquo ; I already feel like I&amp;rsquo ; m part of the community . &amp;rdquo ; Fleming&amp;rsquo ; s partner Amanda Kellock is the artistic director of Repercussion Theatre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hudson Village Theatre is at 28 Wharf Rd . Individual tickets cost $35 , plus tax and service charges , a three-play pass costs $114.66 or a four-play pass costs $144.82 . NewsAfghanistan veteran channels his demons -- but also joy -- through musichttp : **35;31295;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;31332;TOOLONG ... title= " Ryan Carey , a military veteran and singer/songwriter , is performing a solo concert , Feb. 22 in Montreal . " alt= " Ryan Carey , a military veteran and singer/songwriter , is performing a solo concert , Feb. 22 in Montreal . " data-portal-copyright= " John Mahoney " **28;31367;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1723026 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Military veteran Ryan Carey is not one to sidestep difficulty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the CFL , he played in the Grey Cup . Then he joined the military and was sent to Afghanistan . He returned from the brutal tour with PTSD . At first he hid his symptoms , feeling isolated and ashamed , until a colleague twigged to the panic attacks and urged Carey to seek help . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As his therapy moved forward , Carey turned to music , and soon playing guitar and writing songs took a prominent place on his journey toward better mental health . He wanted to help others discover the healing nature of music , so he began to teach guitar to &lt;a href= " https : **33;31397;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;veterans at the Ste-Anne Hospital&lt;/a&gt; in Ste-Anne-de-Bellevue , as part of the Guitar for Vets program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey wrote songs about combat , pain and anger , and then dug deep into his surplus of courage to perform at open-mic nights and &lt;a href= " https : **33;31432;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;at the last Hudson Porchfest&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;At the urging of a fellow vet , Carey is taking his music one big step farther , with an evening of original and cover songs , called Coming Home to You : What It 's Like to Go to War , at the Royal Montreal Regiment , Saturday . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div id= " fb-root " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;script async= " 1 " defer= " 1 " crossorigin= " anonymous " src= " https : **33;31467;TOOLONG ... class= " fb-video " data-href= " https : **28;31502;TOOLONG ... data-width= " 640 " &gt;&lt;blockquote cite= " https : **33;31532;TOOLONG ... class= " fb-xfbml-parse-ignore " &gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;31567;TOOLONG ... Kiss My Son&lt;/a&gt;&lt;p&gt;I KISS MY SON&lt;/p&gt;Posted by &lt;a href= " https : **33;31602;TOOLONG ... Carey&lt;/a&gt; on Thursday , June 14 , **35;31637;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m scared as hell , but I&amp;rsquo ; m doing it anyway , &amp;rdquo ; Carey said . &amp;ldquo ; Every day I write in my journal : &amp;lsquo ; As I create and listen , I will be led. &amp;rsquo ; My songs are about the dark side of the human psyche . Being stuck in grief is really hard . You can lose yourself , going down that rabbit hole . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey will share the stage with fellow veteran Michael Terry , who launched Dispatches , a cross-Canada motorcycle ride , in 2018 to talk to first responders and veterans about his story and how it is crucial to seek help . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; ( Michael ) is very honest and open about his PTSD , &amp;rdquo ; Carey said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey has fashioned his two-part program after the funeral-procession traditions of New Orleans . A funeral&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; first line&amp;rdquo ; is sombre &amp;mdash ; with family members and the hearse leading the way . Its &amp;ldquo ; second line&amp;rdquo ; is a celebration &amp;mdash ; with music , singing and dancing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And so the second part of the evening is about love and humour , &amp;rdquo ; Carey said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The veteran is in a much better place these days , but his recovery is ongoing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think I am more honest and open with the people in my life , &amp;rdquo ; Carey said . &amp;ldquo ; I no longer let depression or anxiety ruin my life . I journal , meditate , exercise , do breathing exercises . I have a healthy mantra , I eat healthy food , I work on healthy relationships . I would say I am in a comfortable place . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey absolutely believes music saved his life and said if his performance helps one other veteran step out of the shadows , he will consider it a success . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey is also putting his songwriting skills to the test by submitting songs to the Golden Pick Songwriting Competition , organized by the A Song Is Forever Foundation . The ASIFF is an outreach organization that encourages vets and first responders dealing with operational stress injuries to engage in creative expression through music . Winners get to work with a professional songwriter and possibly record . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Carey appears in the Isabelle Raynault documentary De la musique pour la cerveau . The film looks at the latest in research into the neurological healing effects of music and is playing at the Cin&amp;eacute ; ma du mus&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Coming Home to You : &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;What It 's Like to Go to War&lt;/span&gt; is at the Royal Montreal Regiment , 4625 Ste-Catherine St. W. , Feb. 22 at 7 p.m .
