Full story &lt;a href= " https : **33;32725;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;here&lt;/a&gt;. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div class= " entry-content " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; Montreal GazetteMon , 17 Feb 2020 13:16:36 **45;32760;TOOLONG ... review : Post Malone proves he 's rap 's rock starhttp : **36;32807;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;32845;TOOLONG ... title= " Singer/rapper Post Malone performs in concert at the Bell Centre in Montreal , Feb. 16 , 2020. " alt= " Singer/rapper Post Malone performs in concert at the Bell Centre in Montreal , Feb. 16 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Christinne Muschi " **28;32880;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1724082 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; American chart topper and streaming sensation Post Malone&amp;rsquo ; s headlining Bell Centre set on Sunday night aspired to be a rock show in rap clothing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In front of a rowdy , youthful crowd emboldened by his calls for drunken revelry and clamouring for some of radio&amp;rsquo ; s stickiest , most insidious hooks , Malone emulated his favourite emo and rock frontmen with his mic-stand-straddling mannerisms and throaty wails . But the musical backdrop was pure pop-trap anatomically built to shatter Spotify records , and the resulting combination proved too irresistible for most in the audience , even as the patterns became overly familiar during the 90-minute set . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Malone , dressed casually in a white t-shirt and jeans , flied solo on a thin catwalk , only accompanied by a backing track . He opened with the title track from 2019 album Hollywood&amp;rsquo ; s Bleeding , which pairs a hip hop beat with melancholic guitar . It&amp;rsquo ; s a prevalent mix both on the album and this current tour , but with the low-end still reigning in the mix , the increased use of guitar felt like more of an aesthetic decision than a left turn toward rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll . Malone was likely a keen pupil of the rapcore scene of the late &amp;rsquo ; 90s/early &amp;rsquo ; 00s , but while those bands were rockers at heart with some rap flourishes thrown in , Malone was born in the era of rap and sees rock as something to reverse engineer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Where Malone has always identified more as a rocker , besides his affinity for emo posturing and frat boy arrested development , is in attitude/antics ( as he states in less graceful terms on Rockstar ) . On Sunday , he described his songs to the crowd as being about &amp;ldquo ; feeling like s*** and having your heart broken , &amp;rdquo ; and one got the sense that even though he&amp;rsquo ; s using hip hop tools and expressions , Malone&amp;rsquo ; s key to success in the mainstream has been plugging in to tried and true suburban angst of his favourite rock heroes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Better Now from 2018&amp;rsquo ; s Beerbongs and Bentleys and Saint-Tropez early on in the set demonstrated his melodic chops and ability to rap and sing in equal measure , Malone took stylistic chances with the punk pop and cheerleader-influenced Allergic and cartoon bubbly rap of Candy Paint , but the subtleties of both got lost in the omnipresent bass . That was followed by Psycho , one of Malone&amp;rsquo ; s signature tunes with an obvious singalong element the crowd enthusiastically telegraphed . Wow from Hollywood&amp;rsquo ; s Bleeding was pure pop rap . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Malone was joined virtually by an all-too-brief video cameo from Ozzy Osbourne , who guested on the album track Take What You Want . Inspired by the guitar solo that ends the song and that also appeared here in pre-recorded form , Malone delivered a heavy metal scream with gusto and donned an acoustic guitar for Stay . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can&amp;rsquo ; t play guitar like the solo on the last song , but can I play some guitar ? &amp;rdquo ; Malone asked the audience before admitting in Malonian fashion that he played a ton of beer pong prior the show . He returned to a guitar sample &amp;mdash ; a dead ringer for Broken Social Scene or America , depending on your age &amp;mdash ; for the &amp;rsquo ; 70s soft rock ode Circles , his most realized rap-rock hybrid yet . After his earliest hit White Iverson and Spider-Man soundtrack cut Sunflower , Malone smashed his acoustic guitar and doused it with Bud Light at the conclusion of the aforementioned Rockstar. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He concluded the night with Congratulations from 2016 breakthrough album Stoney , and while it remains one of his most memorable hooks , after 90 minutes of Malone returning to the same vocal flows again and again , as catchy as they were , one ended the evening wishing for a little more spontaneity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;32910;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;32952;TOOLONG ... flash reaction turns Post Malone into **25;32987;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;33014;TOOLONG ... Malone sent emotional goodbye text during plane **30;33049;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Erik Leijon , Special to Montreal GazetteMon , 17 Feb 2020 12:49:44 **45;33081;TOOLONG ... enlightenment seekers at work in Small Mouth Soundshttp : **34;33128;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;33164;TOOLONG ... title= " Warona Setshwaelo , left , Gabe Grey , Alison Darcy , Matthew Gagnon and Andreas Apergis cut the cackle in Small Mouth Sounds . " alt= " Warona Setshwaelo , left , Gabe Grey , Alison Darcy , Matthew Gagnon and Andreas Apergis cut the cackle in Small Mouth Sounds . " data-portal-copyright= " Leslie Schachter " **28;33199;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721578 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Life is suffering&amp;rdquo ; goes the Buddhist epigraph that opens the printed version of Bess Wohl&amp;rsquo ; s enjoyably quirky tragicomedy Small Mouth Sounds , playing at the Segal Studio to March 1 . But deep in the woods , no one can hear you scream . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s because all verbal communication is forbidden during the five-day silent retreat that is the setting for the play . It&amp;rsquo ; s presided over by the amplified voice of the unseen Teacher ( Marcelo Arroyo ) , who makes up for the silence with amusingly platitudinous parables and asinine observations about cold medicines and his own boring dreams . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Much of the fun lies in watching the reactions of his clients , a motley sextet who sit in a row of chairs dutifully taking notes or squirming in silent rebellion . When class is dismissed , they go off to wordlessly squabble in their spartan cabins or get into scrapes in the surrounding nature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s something Chekhovian about Bruno-Pierre Houle&amp;rsquo ; s gorgeous set design , with its expanse of wooden decking and autumnal trees . But it&amp;rsquo ; s Tolstoy&amp;rsquo ; s famous quote about families that comes to mind as we watch these seekers of enlightenment all being unhappy in their own way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joan ( Alison Darcy ) and Judy ( Warona Setshwaelo ) are a couple whose relationship is buckling under the strain of an illness . More immediately , one of them is less convinced than the other by all the New Agey trippiness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rodney ( Gabe Grey ) is a celebrity yoga teacher with a misplaced confidence in his own mindfulness . Alicia ( Zara Jestadt ) is a minor actress struggling with a breakup , panic attacks and an un-Zen-like tendency to make as much noise as possible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the play&amp;rsquo ; s wordiest section , Ned , one of life&amp;rsquo ; s losers , hits the microphone and lists all the disasters that have befallen him . It&amp;rsquo ; s a very funny monologue with an existentially terrifying payoff and is beautifully delivered by Matthew Gagnon with a hangdog shrug . Jan ( Andreas Apergis ) , the most enigmatic character , turns out to have perhaps the most tragic backstory . He also has a double-whammy of climactic twists , by turns delightfully funny and achingly sad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even the teacher , who receives most of Wohl&amp;rsquo ; s satirical barbs , is ultimately drawn with a generosity of spirit , paying tribute to his sincere solicitousness for his clients and his agonized doubts over whether he&amp;rsquo ; s doing them any good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite the temptation for silent knockabout comedy , the humour is mostly gentle . Director Caitlin Murphy and her cast keep things nicely focused and the characters clearly drawn ( though a couple of moments of over-compensatory mugging threaten to break the spell ) . Clarity is added by the discriminating shifts of Martin Sirois&amp;rsquo ; s lighting and by Sophie El Assaad&amp;rsquo ; s subtly inventive costume design . Rob Denton&amp;rsquo ; s evocative natural soundscape and serene incidental music manage to stop the silences from getting too awkward . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At a brisk , interval-less 90 minutes , it&amp;rsquo ; s a quietly entertaining evening . But I did wonder if it could have risked a more contemplative pace now and then , if only to immerse us in that silence , and the play&amp;rsquo ; s themes , all the more deeply . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Small Mouth Sounds plays to March 1 at the Segal Studio Theatre , 5170 C ? te-Ste-Catherine-Rd . Tickets $45 . Student and under-30 discounts available . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33382;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1722625 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; No , Homer Simpson won&amp;rsquo ; t cut it . Nor will Don Cherry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Apparently , cartoon characters aren&amp;rsquo ; t eligible to appear on the next $5 Canadian bank note , to replace this country&amp;rsquo ; s first francophone prime minister , Sir Wilfrid Laurier. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , we&amp;rsquo ; re only talking Homer here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even if Homer weren&amp;rsquo ; t a cartoon character , he would still be disqualified because he isn&amp;rsquo ; t a Canadian by birth or naturalization . Furthermore , there would be considerable doubt in certain quarters that Homer has demonstrated &amp;ldquo ; outstanding leadership , achievement or distinction in any field&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; certainly not on the nuclear energy front where he has toiled &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; benefiting the people of Canada or in the service of Canada . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The latter are some of the other determining factors for an acceptable candidate , as laid out by the Bank of Canada , which heads the search for a new face for our fiver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for Cherry , forget all the aforementioned Bank of Canada criteria , he hasn&amp;rsquo ; t been dead for at least 25 years . ( Indeed , he still lives. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This last qualification has been the big stumbling block for many who&amp;rsquo ; ve already submitted suggestions &amp;mdash ; and for which Homer and Cherry have been mentioned . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It disqualifies other suggested Canucks like the still breathing Drake , &lt;a href= " https : **33;33412;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;C ? line Dion&lt;/a&gt; , Avril Lavigne and Shawn Mendes . Same too for rocket-man Chris Hadfield and hockey superstars Wayne Gretzky and Bobby Orr. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sadly , it also precludes Habs legends Jean B&amp;eacute ; liveau and Maurice Richard , who died after 1995 . Same , too , for such deserving arts legends as Gord Downie and &lt;a href= " https : **33;33447;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Leonard Cohen&lt;/a&gt; . Also missing out is human-rights advocate Mary Two-Axe Earley , who died in 1996. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No worries . There is still an abundance of Canadians who more than meet all the necessary criteria . Serious consideration would have to be given to Tommy Douglas , the ex-New Democratic Party leader and father of Medicare , and Terry Fox , whose courageous one-legged marathon odyssey raised cancer awareness like never before in this country . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Bank of Canada made a similar call for nominations in 2016 for a new face to grace our $10 note . That resulted in the &lt;a href= " http : **34;33482;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;popular selection of Viola Desmond&lt;/a&gt; , the inspiring human rights activist who challenged racial segregation in Nova Scotia last century . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It would be nice to have another Canadian woman grace a note . Certainly , the equally formidable Montreal feminist and politician Th&amp;eacute ; r&amp;egrave ; se Casgrain , Agnes Macphail , Canada&amp;rsquo ; s first female Member of Parliament , and author , social activist , suffragette , politician Nellie McClung deserve serious consideration . Also among those meriting attention are hometown poet &amp;Eacute ; mile Nelligan and authors Margaret Laurence and Lucy Maud Montgomery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the male cultural front , lest we forget such world-class pioneers as actor/activist Chief Dan George , pianist Glenn Gould , artist A.Y . Jackson , author Stephen Leacock , filmmaker Norman McLaren or media philosopher Marshall McLuhan ( man , would he be having a field-day now ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And what about Renaissance Man Sir Frederick Banting , the physician , scientist , painter , Nobel laureate and co-discoverer of insulin who makes pretty much everyone else look like an underachieving slacker ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the chuckles front , there have been few funnier than John Candy , as good-hearted a soul as any and as much appreciated elsewhere on the planet as here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A case could be made for the Wayne and Shuster comedy tandem , save that while Johnny Wayne died in 1990 , Frank Shuster went 12 years later and is thus ineligible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And , what the hey , for your consideration : the pride of **25;33518;TOOLONG , Louis Cyr , reputed to be the strongest human who ever lived . He did , after all , lift 500 pounds with a single finger . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; True , there&amp;rsquo ; s also Tim Horton , one-time hockey star and founder of the fast-food empire producing Homer Simpson&amp;rsquo ; s favourite vegetable &amp;mdash ; that&amp;rsquo ; s right , the &amp;hellip ; mmm&amp;hellip ; doughnut. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oh yeah , and here are few brain-teasing questions for you . No cheating . Without going into your wallet or bank to check , who graces our $20 , $50 and $100 notes ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hint : one isn&amp;rsquo ; t even a Canadian . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yes , that would be Queen Elizabeth II on the $20 , and she is there for life or until a new king is crowned . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , honestly , how many of you can say that you knew it was Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 10th prime minister , William Lyon Mackenzie King &amp;mdash ; don&amp;rsquo ; t call him Mac &amp;mdash ; on the $50 or our eighth prime minister , Sir Robert Laird Borden , as the face of our $100 ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regardless , since the Bank of Canada is giving the boot to seventh prime minister Laurier , perhaps the bank should also say bye-bye to Borden and Mackenzie King . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , by the time that happens , coins could well replace bills with foonies and doonies , et al . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Up to five nominations per form for your choice to appear on the Canadian $5 note must be submitted by March 11 at &lt;a href= " https : **33;33545;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;33580;TOOLONG ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **32;33613;TOOLONG " **39;33647;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **28;33688;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;33718;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Bill Brownstein , Montreal GazetteSat , 15 Feb 2020 11:00:37 **45;33750;TOOLONG ... " alt= " Streamer Brittany Heim practises on Void Edge , the new edition of Rainbow Six Siege , at Ubisoft on Thursday Feb. 13 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;33869;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1722169 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Call it shootout at Place Bell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Friday through Sunday , 4,500 gaming enthusiasts will pay $135 to $240 a ticket to watch the world&amp;rsquo ; s top 16 teams compete to be named world champion of Ubisoft&amp;rsquo ; s tactical shooter game Rainbow Six Siege . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A total of $3 million in prize money will be handed out at the end of the Six Invitational weekend , with the winning team claiming $1 million , or $200,000 per player . The event also features developer panels , cosplayers and talks by content creators from the gaming community . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The teams competing hail from countries including Brazil , Australia , France , Ukraine , Spain , the U.K. and the U.S. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the biggest ( Rainbow Six ) Esport event of the year , &amp;rdquo ; said lead game designer Jean-Baptiste Hall&amp;eacute ; at Ubisoft&amp;rsquo ; s Mile End headquarters on Thursday afternoon . &amp;ldquo ; Last year , we had 22 million unique viewers watching around the world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tom Clancy&amp;rsquo ; s Rainbow Six Siege is a first-person tactical shooter video game developed by Ubisoft , released on Dec. 1 , 2015 . The game emphasizes team play , putting players in hostile environments where they alternate between being attackers and defenders in situations with hostages , bombs or an area to be secured . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are more than 55 million Rainbow Six Siege players around the world . Players can either partake with friends or let the game choose teammates for them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Environmental destruction is a key component to the game . An introductory video reminds players that &amp;ldquo ; walls , floors and ceilings can be breached . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;33899;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;33935;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Players can choose from dozens of &amp;ldquo ; operators , &amp;rdquo ; i.e. counterterrorist avatars to incarnate , each with their own special skills , some specializing in offence and others in defence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Teams can select from international counterterrorism units including the FBI SWAT ( Hostage Rescue Team ) , the British SAS , Germany&amp;rsquo ; s GSG-9 , Russia&amp;rsquo ; s Spetsnaz and France&amp;rsquo ; s GIGN. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are five players per team . The multiplayer format has helped breed a sense of community among gamers and made the game stand out from others in the genre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Fundamentally , it was a wager ( for Ubisoft ) , &amp;rdquo ; Hall&amp;eacute ; said . &amp;ldquo ; We were used to doing single-player games with stories . This is very different &amp;mdash ; multiplayer is more risky . Many years later , I can say it was a nice surprise . When you embark on something like this , there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of luck involved . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Providing players with predefined characters to choose from &amp;mdash ; as opposed to allowing them to create their own &amp;mdash ; was also a switch from the status quo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In most other shooter games by our competitors , you customize your character , &amp;rdquo ; Hall&amp;eacute ; said . &amp;ldquo ; Here the take is different . Each character has a name and a story . So when you take Thermite , other players can&amp;rsquo ; t &amp;mdash ; because there&amp;rsquo ; s only one . That changes a lot . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rainbow Six Siege is highly competitive , according to Hall&amp;eacute ; , and stakes are high because of what he calls a &amp;ldquo ; strong lethality &amp;mdash ; you die quickly when you get shot . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The game&amp;rsquo ; s team leader , Six , is voiced by Angela Bassett . Creators studied historical hostage situations for inspiration , including London&amp;rsquo ; s Iranian Embassy siege of 1980 , the Lufthansa Flight 181 hijacking in 1977 and the 2002 Moscow theatre hostage crisis . They also consulted with real counterterrorism units . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;33971;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34013;TOOLONG ... of jobs ' promised as video game firm 2K sets up studio in **29;34048;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34079;TOOLONG ... Canada wants to grow player count to 200 million by **25;34114;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34141;TOOLONG ... had to act quickly when For Honor players started jumping **25;34176;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is a variety of maps of each environment , which players can study to increase their chances of survival . Each locale requires teams to adapt their strategy , tactics and operators accordingly : bank , chalet and consulate were three examples of environments listed on the Ubisoft website. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of Rainbow Six Siege&amp;rsquo ; s trademark features is the utter destructibility of its environments . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We use advanced techniques to allow you to pulverize walls in analogical ways , &amp;rdquo ; Hall&amp;eacute ; said . &amp;ldquo ; The holes that form ( as a result ) can be infinitely varied . Technically , that&amp;rsquo ; s very difficult to do . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like a Netflix series on overdrive , the game is divided into years and seasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Year 4 , Season 3 , for example , was released in September . Titled Operation Ember Rise , it introduces two new operators , Amaru and Goyo , from Peru and Mexico . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Year 5 , Season 1 is being released this weekend , with more surprises in store . Video game streamers and influencers were at Ubisoft on Thursday testing out the new edition , Void Edge , under embargo before the big reveal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All in all , it&amp;rsquo ; s shaping up to be quite the weekend for fans of Rainbow Six Siege and the people behind it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a big morale boost for the team , &amp;rdquo ; Hall&amp;eacute ; said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve worked hard all year , without necessarily being in contact with the community of people who play the game . Now we find ourselves immersed with the fans . It&amp;rsquo ; s very energizing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **27;34203;TOOLONG **27;34232;TOOLONG takes place Friday to Sunday at Place Bell in Laval . Doors open at 9 a.m. each day , playoffs start at noon . Tickets cost $135 for an " operator pass " to $240 for an " elite pass " ( elite passes are sold out ) . The event can also be watched on &lt;a href= " https : //www.twitch.tv/rainbow6 " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;twitch.tv&lt;/a&gt; or &lt;a href= " https : **33;34261;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;YouTube&lt;/a&gt; . ArtsLocal BusinessLocal NewsBrownstein : Assaf Havilio found 200 languages to tell his wife he loves herhttp : **37;34540;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;34579;TOOLONG ... title= " Are you just buying a card ? Awwwww . Assaf Havilio wrote his family this book , I Love You in Every Language . " alt= " Are you just buying a card ? Awwwww . Assaf Havilio wrote his family this book , I Love You in Every Language . " data-portal-copyright= " John Mahoney " **28;34614;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1720928 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;34644;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Quite possibly , it could give one pause if someone were to utter &amp;ldquo ; Asavakkit&amp;rdquo ; in one&amp;rsquo ; s ear . Same with &amp;ldquo ; Ik zie je graag. &amp;rdquo ; And most definitely with &amp;ldquo ; Rwy&amp;rsquo ; n dy garu di . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , the latter mouthful might even lead to fisticuffs if misconstrued. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But harsh as these words may sound to some , they are actually heart-felt declarations meaning &amp;ldquo ; I love you&amp;rdquo ; in , respectively , Greenlandic , Flemish Dutch and Welsh . Admittedly they are not nearly as soothing to the ear as the Italian &amp;ldquo ; Ti amo , &amp;rdquo ; the French &amp;ldquo ; Je t&amp;rsquo ; aime&amp;rdquo ; or the ever-catchy Kenyan Swahili &amp;ldquo ; Nakupenda , &amp;rdquo ; but they still do the trick . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Every day is Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day for Montreal graphic designer Assaf Havilio . So to demonstrate his passion for his wife and their four kids year-round , Havilio decided to pen and design a book for them , I Love You in Every Language . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beautifully illustrated by Denis Proulx , it not only includes over 200 takes on &amp;ldquo ; I Love You , &amp;rdquo ; as spoken everywhere from Afghanistan to Zimbabwe , but it also offers other tender phrases in an array of languages and explains the genesis of this project . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s a whole lotta love . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The background for the book&amp;rsquo ; s story features two cartoon characters , Mr. Explorer and Miss Languages , modeled after Havilio and his wife , Merav , a former language teacher . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beyond his feelings for his family , Havilio was also motivated by &amp;ldquo ; perhaps a simple desire in search of peace in the world . &amp;rdquo ; Born in Jerusalem , Havilio , who moved here 12 years ago , knows all too well about living in a volatile tinderbox. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If nothing else , one learns to understand and appreciate the concept of peace when it can be so difficult to attain , &amp;rdquo ; Havilio , 45 , says , over a caf&amp;eacute ; latte &amp;mdash ; with , appropriately , a heart emblazoned on top &amp;mdash ; at Avenue G. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **32;34680;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;34714;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I then figured a great way for people from all cultures and countries to understand one another is simply to learn how to say these three words in every language . It couldn&amp;rsquo ; t hurt . Love does , after all , connect most people and is universal . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The concept for the book first came to him while visiting India and Nepal a few decades back . Upon further travels in Japan , Singapore and Thailand , he decided to pursue the project . But the idea was really cemented here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It turns out that at least 50 of the languages referred to in the book are spoken in this city alone . This is really one of the most fascinating multicultural places in the world , although many people seem to take that for granted here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Since moving here , I&amp;rsquo ; ve made friends with people who&amp;rsquo ; ve come from all over the world , from Iran , India , South America and Africa . It&amp;rsquo ; s interesting how living here has really opened up my eyes to the rest of the world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although he has an idea for a follow-up book , Havilio has no notions of giving up his day job as a graphic designer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have no fantasies about making my fortune with this book , &amp;rdquo ; he says with a broad smile . &amp;ldquo ; It has truly been a labour of love . I just wanted to pass on something to my wife and kids , and it has turned into this great adventure , which has taken me more than 10 years to complete . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My reward has been people telling me they see the book as both an educational tool for kids and a good Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day offering . That&amp;rsquo ; s more than enough . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;34750;TOOLONG ... title= " Are you just buying a card for Valentine 's Day ? Awwwww . Assaf Havilio wrote his family a book , the title of which he 's wearing on this T-shirt . " alt= " Are you just buying a card for Valentine 's Day ? Awwwww . Assaf Havilio wrote his family a book , the title of which he 's wearing on this T-shirt . " data-portal-copyright= " John Mahoney " **28;34785;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1720927 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Havilio plans to go with &amp;ldquo ; Je t&amp;rsquo ; aime&amp;rdquo ; to his wife on Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day . Then press the tip of his nose onto hers and softly declare the Inuit version , &amp;ldquo ; Negligevapse. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think I will also go with the Romani term &amp;lsquo ; Kamauhtut. &amp;rsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m still so intrigued by all the different sounds for love . I always thought of myself as a romantic , but I&amp;rsquo ; ve taken it to a whole new level now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Still , the book is a great present for now and my wife has been very understanding , yet I realize I won&amp;rsquo ; t be able to use it every year as a Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day gift . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Havilio notes that he and his family are still learning to adjust to these polar parts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Winters are so long for those of us who come from more tropical places , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; But a peaceful environment will always compensate for a lack of heat outside . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;I Love You in Every Language , written and designed by Assaf Havilio and illustrated by Denis Proulx , &lt;/em&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34815;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;is available at amazon.ca&lt;/a&gt; . Price is $20. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **32;34850;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **39;34884;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **28;34925;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;34955;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;34987;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35029;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsRelationshipsThen we take Berlin : Quebec filmmakers swarm Berlinalehttp : **31;35209;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;35242;TOOLONG ... title= " " After the initial shock , I felt very lucky , " Philippe Falardeau says of learning his film My Salinger Year will open the Berlinale . " Berlin has a nice balance of ( crowd-pleasing ) and edgier films . My film does n't qualify as edgy , but it certainly qualifies as an artistic statement . " " alt= " " After the initial shock , I felt very lucky , " Philippe Falardeau says of learning his film My Salinger Year will open the Berlinale . " Berlin has a nice balance of ( crowd-pleasing ) and edgier films . My film does n't qualify as edgy , but it certainly qualifies as an artistic statement . " " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;35277;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721734 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not as glamorous as Cannes , but it&amp;rsquo ; s almost as prestigious . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Berlinale , a.k.a. the Berlin International Film Festival , is one of a handful of top-tier cinematic events in the world . So it&amp;rsquo ; s with great enthusiasm that an array of Canadian filmmakers will descend on the &amp;uuml ; ber-hip German cultural hub , and Quebec is leading the way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Philippe Falardeau was stunned to learn his latest feature , My Salinger Year , had been chosen as the Berlinale&amp;rsquo ; s opening film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It felt a bit unreal , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t quite understand what it meant . Sometimes opening films can be a bit tricky . At the Cannes Film Festival , they&amp;rsquo ; re often big commercial films and it&amp;rsquo ; s more about who&amp;rsquo ; s going to be there and who is willing to pay for the opening party . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But at Berlin , it&amp;rsquo ; s a different thing . I was very moved to see the list of directors who had opening films at the Berlinale in the past 10 years &amp;mdash ; the Coen Brothers , Wes Anderson , Isabel Coixet . I&amp;rsquo ; m just starting to grasp what it means for the film . We&amp;rsquo ; re still looking to sell it in the U.S. , and this will of course help tremendously . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Based on the memoir by Joanna Rakoff , My Salinger Year is a coming-of-age tale about a young woman ( Margaret Qualley ) who moves to New York and finds work as an assistant to J.D. Salinger&amp;rsquo ; s literary agent ( Sigourney Weaver ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Falardeau first went to Berlin in 2009 , where his film C&amp;rsquo ; est pas moi , je le jure ! screened in the Generation Kplus section , winning the Crystal Bear and the Grand Prix . Over a decade later , he&amp;rsquo ; s thrilled to be back . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; After the initial shock , I felt very lucky , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Berlin has a nice balance of ( crowd-pleasing ) and edgier films . My film doesn&amp;rsquo ; t qualify as edgy , but it certainly qualifies as an artistic statement . And the selection confirms that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Luc D&amp;eacute ; ry , who co-produced the film with his microscope partner Kim McCraw , opening the Berlinale is a dream come true . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a big deal , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a big spotlight on the film , and we&amp;rsquo ; re really happy . Berlin is recognized as the festival of great auteurs . I would say it&amp;rsquo ; s ( artier than ) Cannes , Toronto , Venice and Sundance , which are recognized as the four big film festivals of the world . This is a film that is arty , with big commercial potential . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; D&amp;eacute ; ry and McCraw have a second film at the Berlinale : Pomp&amp;eacute ; i , by Anna Falgu&amp;egrave ; res and John Shank . The Belgian-French-Canadian co-production co-stars Quebec actor Aliocha Schneider , and premi&amp;egrave ; red at the Toronto International Film Festival in September . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This week Quebec director Ana&amp;iuml ; s Barbeau-Lavalette was putting the finishing touches on her new film , La d&amp;eacute ; esse des mouches &amp;agrave ; feu , based on the novel of the same name by Genevi&amp;egrave ; ve Pettersen . It will have its world premi&amp;egrave ; re in competition in the Berlinale&amp;rsquo ; s Generation 14plus section . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the only ( major ) festival in the world that ( has a special section ) for a youth audience , &amp;rdquo ; she said , while noting that although her film features a teenage protagonist growing up in the &amp;rsquo ; 90s , it&amp;rsquo ; s not aimed at young people per se. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It talks about adolescence , &amp;rdquo ; she said , &amp;ldquo ; but it&amp;rsquo ; s also for adults who were once adolescents . It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t moralize , or talk down to the audience . It&amp;rsquo ; s very in-your-face . There are sex scenes , drugs , and I broach them the way I did as an adolescent &amp;mdash ; not being scared of them , but wanting to explore . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Barbeau-Lavalette has been to the Berlinale twice before , to present her films Le ring , in 2008 , and Inch&amp;rsquo ; Allah , in 2014. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a festival I respect profoundly , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Having travelled to other festivals , I can say that this is a festival that , despite its grandeur and its reputation , has retained an authenticity that resembles the city . It&amp;rsquo ; s a festival for film lovers and cinematic storytelling . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;35307;TOOLONG ... title= " " It 's a festival I respect profoundly , " Ana ? s Barbeau-Lavalette says of the Berlinale . " This is a festival that , despite its grandeur and its reputation , has retained an authenticity that resembles the city . " " alt= " " It 's a festival I respect profoundly , " Ana ? s Barbeau-Lavalette says of the Berlinale . " This is a festival that , despite its grandeur and its reputation , has retained an authenticity that resembles the city . " " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;35342;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721736 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Montrealer Matthew Rankin stretches the limits of storytelling with &lt;a href= " https : **33;35372;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Twentieth Century&lt;/a&gt; , his far-out biopic of former Canadian prime minister William Lyon Mackenzie King . The film , which premi&amp;egrave ; red at TIFF in September , screens in the Berlinale&amp;rsquo ; s experimental Forum section . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s for films that are pushing the boundaries of the form , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really punk rock . I feel like it&amp;rsquo ; s a great fit , like the audience will be ready for something strange . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rankin attended the festival in 2011 , when he was chosen to be part of Berlinale Talents , a program for emerging filmmakers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a festival I deeply admire , &amp;rdquo ; he said , &amp;ldquo ; and the Forum program is one I love . I&amp;rsquo ; ve followed it for a number of years . A lot of filmmakers I adore have unveiled films there . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rankin is excited to be attending the festival with his friend , Toronto director Kazik Radwanski , whose Anne at 13,000 Ft. also screens in the Forum section . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There will be a Canadian Indigenous contingent at the Berlinale this year , beginning with veteran Abenaki filmmaker &lt;a href= " https : **33;35407;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Alanis Obomsawin&lt;/a&gt; , who will serve on the jury of the documentary section . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It certainly has a lot of power and prestige , &amp;rdquo ; Obomsawin said of the festival , &amp;ldquo ; and a lot of filmmakers are happy to have their films there . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Montreal-based non-profit film workshop organization Wapikoni Mobile will present six Indigenous shorts in various sections of the Berlinale , including young Inuit filmmakers Eva Kaukai and Manon Chamberland&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;35442;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Throat Singing in Kangirsuk&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Reflecting on all the Canadian talent at the Berlinale this year , Telefilm Canada executive director Christa Dickenson was suitably impressed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot to be proud of , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; The Berlin International Film Festival is one of those top marquee festivals . We&amp;rsquo ; ve got 16 Canadian projections as part of the Berlinale program , five of which were supported by Telefilm directly . This is an opportunity for Canadian filmmaking talent to shine . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **27;35477;TOOLONG 70th Berlinale&lt;/strong&gt; runs from Feb. 20 through March 1 . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;35506;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;35537;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;35575;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;35602;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;35635;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35677;TOOLONG ... do so many Quebec films make it into the Oscar live-action short category ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35712;TOOLONG ... brain loves music : Montreal doc shows healing power of **25;35747;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35774;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMontreal moviesNewsMcCord Museum 's graceful Griffintown photographs find logic amid chaoshttp : **35;35951;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;35988;TOOLONG ... title= " " My photographs are like puzzles that you have to figure out , with lots of spatial ambiguities , " says Robert Walker . " alt= " " My photographs are like puzzles that you have to figure out , with lots of spatial ambiguities , " says Robert Walker . " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;36023;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721759 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;36053;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Robert Walker grew up in Hochelaga-Maisonneuve . As the crow flies , that 's not terribly far from Griffintown . But for the boy who was to become one of Montreal 's best-known photographers , the working-class/industrial district to the southwest -- the very place that forms the subject of the McCord Museum 's new one-man show Griffintown : Evolving Montreal -- may as well have been a different world . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " My mother would say , ' Never go there . It 's too dangerous , full of crazy drunken Irishmen , ' " said the son of Scottish immigrants , recalling his childhood in the 1940s and ' 50s . " Anyway , in those days people did n't get around much . You stuck to your own neighbourhood . You stayed on the block . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Fascinated from an early age by cameras , Walker obtained a degree in visual arts from Sir George Williams University , where a pivotal experience was a workshop given by the celebrated American photographer Lee Friedlander . Walker was awakened to the richness and artistic potential of street photography , a form in which he soon broke from the prevailing tradition by choosing to use colour . His work came to international notice via a series of studies of Times Square . Made while living in New York , they demonstrate a flair for what he calls " finding a logic and a rationale in a very chaotic setting . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;It 's a knack demonstrated to great effect in the McCord show , where 20 large-format prints and a slide show of a further 100 shots impose a counterintuitively graceful form on a district that might strike passersby as a case study in urban development run amok . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;36089;TOOLONG ... title= " Griffintown : Evolving Montreal is on display at the McCord Museum through August . " alt= " Griffintown : Evolving Montreal is on display at the McCord Museum through August . " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;36124;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721757 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Little more than a square kilometre in size , and with fewer and fewer visible vestiges of its past , Griffintown presented Walker with special challenges when he was asked to photograph it for the first instalment of the McCord 's planned Evolving Montreal series . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " I was a little bit apprehensive as to whether I 'd find enough subject matter there , " he said . " I assumed it would be a desolate area of boarded-up buildings and warehouses . But when I got down there it turned out to be fantastic , with all kinds of construction and demolition , very interesting contrasts between the 19th century -- the Lachine Canal and the old ( cal ? che ) horse stables -- and the 21st. " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;What strikes the viewer time and again is how Walker 's lens fixes on the things one might normally be tempted to skip over in the search for something more conventionally picturesque : piles of rubble , construction fences , partially obscured machinery looming like malevolent agents of destruction , occasional glimpses of the distant downtown skyline and the mountain providing contrast . It 's a powerful demonstration of the maxim that photography is less about what you shoot than it is about how you frame it , and Walker has a master 's eye . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;36154;TOOLONG ... title= " Robert Walker was initially apprehensive when asked to photograph Griffintown for the McCord Museum , " but when I got down there it turned out to be fantastic , with all kinds of construction and demolition , very interesting contrasts between the 19th century ... and the 21st. " " alt= " Robert Walker was initially apprehensive when asked to photograph Griffintown for the McCord Museum , " but when I got down there it turned out to be fantastic , with all kinds of construction and demolition , very interesting contrasts between the 19th century ... and the 21st. " " data-portal-copyright= " Pierre Obendrauf " **28;36189;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721755 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Working only on clear , sunny days , the better to bring out his subjects ' inherent hues ( " If the colour does n't work , then to me it 's not really a photograph , more just a snapshot " ) , Walker also takes an evident delight in investing his images with layer upon layer of texture and contrast , all of it achieved not through trickery but with a sure sense of composition . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " My photographs are like puzzles that you have to figure out , with lots of spatial ambiguities , " he said . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;It 's borne out in every photograph from the show , with some even incorporating a touch of trompe l'oeil , as when an image of people lounging by a rooftop pool turns out , on closer inspection , to be part of a billboard advertising a condo development yet to be built . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " That 's an idealistic depiction of a scene that , let 's face it , will probably never happen , " Walker said . " I enjoyed showing it alongside what really is happening . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;36219;TOOLONG ... title= " Robert Walker 's powerful images of piles of rubble , construction fences and partially obscured machinery in Griffintown demonstrate the maxim that photography is less about what you shoot than it is about how you frame it . " alt= " Robert Walker 's powerful images of piles of rubble , construction fences and partially obscured machinery in Griffintown demonstrate the maxim that photography is less about what you shoot than it is about how you frame it . " data-portal-copyright= " Robert Walker " **28;36254;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1721758 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;If a touch of social protest might be discerned from that last statement , and from the show in general , Walker claimed it 's not overt -- saying , for example , that he does n't especially regret the loss of swaths of the district 's architectural heritage . What he does regret is what 's replacing it . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " ( Griffintown ) was never all that interesting architecturally . The buildings were pretty banal . But what 's going up now is totally antiseptic -- condo buildings that will never see a ray of sunlight , one unit facing directly onto somebody else 's . Parking is impossible . You never seem to see any children . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " The universities have these urban planning departments -- well , where are they when you need them ? " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;AT A GLANCE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " **29;36284;TOOLONG : Evolving Montreal , &lt;/strong&gt; by Robert Walker , continues through Aug. 9 at the McCord Museum , 690 Sherbrooke St. **26;36315;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The following free events take place during the run : &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Horse Palace , by Nadine Gomez ( Feb. 19 , 6 p.m. ) : &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;A documentary on Montreal 's oldest working stable , soon to disappear from Griffintown with &lt;a href= " https : **33;36343;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the demise of the cal ? che trade&lt;/a&gt;. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The Photobook Club : About Griffintown with Robert Walker ( April 8 , 6 p.m. ) : &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;An open discussion of the photographer 's art , with a focus on the exhibition 's images of Griffintown. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Conversations About the Exhibition ( May 20 , 6 p.m. ) : &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;McCord photography curator H ?
