ArtsBrownstein : Garage in the Point has 180,000 pieces of history , but no fan beltshttp : **35;36810;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;36847;TOOLONG ... title= " Juan Miguel Henriquez Menares , left , and his son , Juan Guillermo Henriquez Steel , own and operate Garage Henriquez , which looks like a museum as much as a garage . " alt= " Juan Miguel Henriquez Menares , left , and his son , Juan Guillermo Henriquez Steel , own and operate Garage Henriquez , which looks like a museum as much as a garage . " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;36882;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1718220 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hmm &amp;hellip ; crossbows from the 1880s , army helmets dating back to the First World War , toy soldiers from Napoleonic times plus an authentic Bonaparte bicorn , British nobility portraits from the 1700s , a parchment of indecipherable origin from the 9th century , vintage cameras , telephones and vitrolas , and a freaky bust of Frankenstein&amp;rsquo ; s screwed-up mug from gosh knows when ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Great , but where are the wiper blades , brake linings and fan belts ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is called Garage Henriquez , right ? Yeah , but Mus&amp;eacute ; e Henriquez would seem far more fitting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Frankly , one could almost more expect to catch a staged duel , between combatants outfitted with some of the antique military pistols and uniforms hanging on the walls , than to see a mechanic perform a lube job or fiddle with piston rings . Really . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This place is a trip , and then some . A trip back in time , too . It is an absolutely eclectic , though eye-popping , cultural oasis on a street known for many things but not for treasures from the past . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This Centre St. auto-repair shop in &lt;a href= " https : **33;36912;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;36947;TOOLONG is the unlikely home of &amp;mdash ; yes &amp;mdash ; more than 180,000 items , which would certainly be the envy of museum curators and antique pickers everywhere . Yet it is also a dizzying display of memorabilia that will leave regular folk of all ages simply gobsmacked. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Oh , wait , I&amp;rsquo ; ve just spotted a gas pump and some tires in one corner of this jam-packed garage . Forget it . Turns out the pump is gas-less and non-functional , a relic from 100 years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The tires are just for my 1914 Ford Model T in back , &amp;rdquo ; explains ever-genial garage/museum owner Juan Henriquez. &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s really no room here to store most parts for cars in need of service . So we call suppliers to deliver them as they are needed . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Makes sense . Sort of . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Henriquez , 59 , moved here from Chile in 1987 and has been toiling as a mechanic for most of his adult life . He has been operating this garage for 13 years , and it is filled with items that he first began collecting when he was 10. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know many 10-year-olds who like this sort of stuff , but I&amp;rsquo ; ve always been so &lt;a href= " https : **33;36980;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;fascinated by history&lt;/a&gt; and antiques and , yes , also cars . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;37015;TOOLONG ... title= " A child 's tricycle in the form of a plane features movie character dolls and hangs from the ceiling at Garage Henriquez in Pointe-St-Charles. " alt= " A child 's tricycle in the form of a plane features movie character dolls and hangs from the ceiling at Garage Henriquez in Pointe-St-Charles. " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;37050;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1718222 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over a relic of a cash register in what passes for the garage office , there is a photo of Henriquez at 17 , next to a Fiat and Mini Cooper that he raced in Chile . He had won his share of trophies as a teen and was told he might have been able to make it on the racing circuit . But he felt he didn&amp;rsquo ; t have the right kind of temperament . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My idea of a great time in a car is taking my Model T for a ride in the summer , &amp;rdquo ; he says with a broad smile , &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the only kind of ticket he might nab in his Model T would be one for going too slow . He kids that it could take him a week to go from 0 to 90 kilometres an hour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is one chill dude , even when delicately picking apart engines of cars in for service . His son , Juan Guillermo , or Juan Jr. , shares his dad&amp;rsquo ; s passion for collecting &amp;mdash ; in his case old toys and comic books &amp;mdash ; and for cars . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; How could I not love antiques ? I&amp;rsquo ; ve been surrounded by them all my life , &amp;rdquo ; Juan Jr. , 34 , says . &amp;ldquo ; But I have to be careful when swinging tools , so I don&amp;rsquo ; t knock out some 300-year-old piece . Or spill oil near the old Montreal Forum seats or the &lt;a href= " https : **33;37080;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;sweaters of Jean B ? liveau&lt;/a&gt; and Sidney Crosby , even though they&amp;rsquo ; re glass-protected. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;37115;TOOLONG ... title= " Gas masks , pilots ' helmets , old telephones and other military gear are on display at Garage Henriquez in the Point . " alt= " Gas masks , pilots ' helmets , old telephones and other military gear are on display at Garage Henriquez in the Point . " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;37150;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1718223 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love when people come here for the first time . Many are so confused and ask if this is a garage or antique shop . So I tell them that it&amp;rsquo ; s both . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But I don&amp;rsquo ; t sell anything here , &amp;rdquo ; Juan Sr. quickly interjects . &amp;ldquo ; This is not about me making money from this . This is my passion . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Adds the grinning Juan Jr. : &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s for sure . It&amp;rsquo ; s about passion not profits for my father . But you really have to respect that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Juan Sr. is a Renaissance man in a mechanic&amp;rsquo ; s smock . His words come out almost like poetry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My satisfaction comes from seeing the eyes of kids and their parents growing wider with such amazement as they wander through here , &amp;rdquo ; Juan Sr. rhapsodizes. &amp;ldquo ; Still , it&amp;rsquo ; s also nice when they want to have work done on their cars . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Longtime customer Patsy Aguilera concedes that she rarely , if ever , hung out in garage service areas before coming here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is so impressive , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve never seen anything like it . I sometimes think I bring my car in more just to look around . There&amp;rsquo ; s always something new , actually old . But don&amp;rsquo ; t forget these are also very good and honest mechanics , which can be very hard to find . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a tiny room , in another corner , Juan Sr. points out &amp;mdash ; no , not his tools , still don&amp;rsquo ; t know where he hides those &amp;mdash ; a mini-Coca-Cola vending-machine museum , including a motor-less one that goes back about 80 years and requires constant ice packed in the bottom . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My mother also loves this stuff , otherwise she never could have lasted all these years with my father , &amp;rdquo ; Juan Jr . jokes . &amp;ldquo ; Still , I&amp;rsquo ; m sure she&amp;rsquo ; s happier he keeps it here and not at their home , otherwise she would never be able to find her way out . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But Juan Jr . believes his dad&amp;rsquo ; s ultimate motivation here is to keep history alive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These days people don&amp;rsquo ; t repair stuff , &amp;rdquo ; Juan Jr . says . &amp;ldquo ; They replace everything from phones to cars . In his own small way , my father just wants to remind people of some of what mankind has created over the centuries , before this stuff is lost and forever forgotten . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And that he has , although hardly in a small way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **32;37180;TOOLONG " **39;37214;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **28;37255;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;37285;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Bill Brownstein , Montreal GazetteMon , 10 Feb 2020 17:59:09 **45;37317;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal BusinessLocal NewsNewsSmall BusinessMontreal jazz fixture Billy Georgette played his last gig at the hospitalhttp : **32;37364;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;37398;TOOLONG ... title= " Musician and historian Billy Georgette was a fixture in the jazz clubs and hotel bars of Montreal for several decades , starting in the early ' 60s playing with bands at Rockhead 's Paradise , the legendary jazz venue at the corner of St-Antoine and Mountain Sts. " alt= " Musician and historian Billy Georgette was a fixture in the jazz clubs and hotel bars of Montreal for several decades , starting in the early ' 60s playing with bands at Rockhead 's Paradise , the legendary jazz venue at the corner of St-Antoine and Mountain Sts. " data-portal-copyright= " Dario Ayala " **28;37433;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1714706 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Billy Georgette&amp;rsquo ; s final concert was on the 14th floor of the Montreal General Hospital in mid-January . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He played a few songs on an old , badly-out-of-tune piano and by the end of the little gig , doctors and nurses came out of their offices to give him a warm round of applause . Four days later , he was dead . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The veteran Montreal jazz musician learned he had lung cancer in December and it spread throughout his body . He died on Jan. 21 at the age of 85. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He played the piano four days before he died , &amp;rdquo ; his sister , Susan Georgette , said in a phone interview . &amp;ldquo ; There was a piano on the 14th floor and I had an orderly help me . We put him in the wheelchair and we wheeled him down ( from the 15th floor where his bed was ) . And the doctors told me , &amp;lsquo ; It hasn&amp;rsquo ; t been tuned in 100 years . &amp;rsquo ; The night before I went , because I can play piano , and I played some chords and it was terribly untuned. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We brought Billy down there and , my God . He first played one of his own compositions , a classical piece , and then he played They Say It&amp;rsquo ; s Wonderful , and then he played a bebop tune . You would not believe how it sounded . It was not out of tune . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; His touch was always so beautiful . And it came through on that piano . And when I looked down that hall , everyone had come out of their rooms , and all the people were just listening . And when I wheeled him out , the whole floor clapped as we walked by . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Susan was crying as she finished recounting the experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her brother was a fixture in the jazz clubs and hotel bars of the city for several decades , starting in the early &amp;rsquo ; 60s playing with bands at Rockhead&amp;rsquo ; s Paradise , the legendary jazz venue at the corner of St-Antoine and Mountain Sts . He would often be the only white musician on stage playing with African-American musicians . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s what the late , great Montreal Gazette columnist Nick Auf der Maur wrote about Georgette in 1986 : &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span class= " doctext " &gt;&lt;span class= " dochighlight " &gt;Billy Georgette&lt;/span&gt; is the epitome of the old trouper. &lt;/span&gt; If you&amp;rsquo ; re not a jazz fan , perhaps you haven&amp;rsquo ; t heard of him . But chances are , if you&amp;rsquo ; ve walked into a hotel or a bar in the Montreal area at some point in the past 30-odd years and heard a piano playing , you&amp;rsquo ; ve seen &lt;span class= " dochighlight " &gt;Billy Georgette&lt;/span&gt; performing . It could be said he&amp;rsquo ; s the dean of Montreal club piano players . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He used to play piano at the old Hotel de la Montagne and Moby Dick&amp;rsquo ; s bars in the 1980s and in more recent years was a regular player at the downtown venue/restaurant House of Jazz ( formerly Biddle&amp;rsquo ; s ) . He played with jazz greats like Mel Torm&amp;eacute ; and Spike Jones , with pop stars like Gordon Lightfoot and Joni Mitchell , and Quebec singers Pauline Julien and Patsy Gallant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Georgette was part of the cool bohemian scene on Mountain , Drummond and Stanley Sts. in the late &amp;rsquo ; 50s and early &amp;rsquo ; 60s , playing at places like the Little Vienna jazz caf&amp;eacute ; . Susan tells the story of the time at the Little Vienna a young Leonard Cohen came up to her brother and asked if he could read some poetry while Georgette and his band played a little bebop . Georgette said &amp;lsquo ; yes&amp;rsquo ; and he and Cohen became good friends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the late &amp;rsquo ; 90s , he formed a group called The Paradise Band along with bass player Errol Walters , drummer Andy Shorter and tenor saxophonist Richard Parris . They started the band and came up with the name when they realized during a conversation at Biddle&amp;rsquo ; s they all had played at Rockhead&amp;rsquo ; s at one time or another . They released an album in 1999 , titled Paradise Band , in tribute to the legendary jazz watering hole . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37463;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37505;TOOLONG ... Last Jam : Paradise regained -- for a **26;37541;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;37569;TOOLONG ... Paradise regained : treasure from Montreal jazz 's golden age&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;37604;TOOLONG ... Through Our Eyes : Sept. 24 , 1981 , Rufus **29;37639;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;37670;TOOLONG ... media presentation tracks the black history of Montreal and **27;37705;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Paradise Band often played the House of Jazz . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He played here for 25 years , &amp;rdquo ; said Jacques Laurin , the day manager at House of Jazz . &amp;ldquo ; In the last 10 years , he was here less often because he&amp;rsquo ; d spend the winters in Florida . He was very well-known . He was really THE source for information about jazz here . If people had to write about Montreal jazz , they&amp;rsquo ; d come see him . He also knew a lot about the history of hockey . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Georgette did extensive research into the history of hockey and the Stanley Cup , eventually writing a musical , Victoria Rink : Le Hockey Enchant&amp;eacute ; , the title referring to the downtown Montreal rink that was reportedly the venue for the first-ever ice-hockey game in 1875. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Georgette was one of the founders of the Society for International Hockey Research and he fought for years to make Montreal the centre of the hockey universe . He wrote an opinion piece for the Gazette in 2002 making the case for building a museum park near the Bell Centre , with a rink for kids , a giant hockey stick and the world&amp;rsquo ; s largest puck . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Billy Georgette is survived by his wife , Marthe Joncas ; his three sons , Zebulon Perron , Willo Perron and Matt Labush ; and his two sisters , Susan Georgette and Linda Paquet . There will be a memorial service at 11 a.m . May 29 at the Mount Royal Funeral Complex , 1297 Foret Rd. , Outremont. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;37734;TOOLONG " **34;37763;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **32;37799;TOOLONG " &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Brendan Kelly , Montreal GazetteSat , 08 Feb 2020 12:30:42 **45;37833;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicA tale of two orchestras : comparing the OSM and Orchestre M ? tropolitainhttp : **27;37880;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **33;37909;TOOLONG ... title= " Ten years ago , Yannick N ? zet-S ? guin was n't a household name among anglo listeners . Today , it would be difficult not to be aware that the music director of the Philadelphia Orchestra and the Metropolitan Opera lives in Montreal . " alt= " Ten years ago , Yannick N ? zet-S ? guin was n't a household name among anglo listeners . Today , it would be difficult not to be aware that the music director of the Philadelphia Orchestra and the Metropolitan Opera lives in Montreal . " data-portal-copyright= " Dario Ayala " **28;37944;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1715997 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;37974;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yannick N&amp;eacute ; zet-S&amp;eacute ; guin and his Orchestre M&amp;eacute ; tropolitain start the second half of their Montreal subscription season Friday with a concert that was sold out weeks ago . Mozart&amp;rsquo ; s Great Mass in C Minor and Jacques H&amp;eacute ; tu&amp;rsquo ; s Symphony No. 5 , if you want to know the particulars . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Well , no kidding . A Yannick sellout in the Maison symphonique is about as hard to predict as a full house in the Bell Centre for the deciding game of a ( quite hypothetical ) Canadiens-Bruins playoff series . Yannick is more than the local boy made good . He is &lt;a href= " https : **33;38010;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the Montreal maestro gone international&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet there is another crowd count , one that invites quiet admiration . &lt;a href= " https : **33;38045;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Kent Nagano&lt;/a&gt; and the OSM sold 92 per cent of available seats to a January Schubert festival that packed six concerts into six days . That would be more than 11,000 tickets . Not many cities can support a sustained outflow of serious symphonic repertoire in a month when potential ticket-buyers are gasping at their credit-card expenses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The big Schubert turnout was not due to a special fascination with this composer . Nagano-led concerts on Feb. 18 and 20 ( Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s Symphony No. 6 &amp;ldquo ; Pastoral&amp;rdquo ; and a newly commissioned Symphony for Organ and Orchestra by the hardcore French modernist&amp;nbsp ; Pascal Dusapin ) are also sold out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The audience is increasing every year , &amp;rdquo ; says OSM chief executive Madeleine Careau. &amp;ldquo ; Not 100,000 people every year , but one , two per cent . It&amp;rsquo ; s quite encouraging for us , especially considering that the cultural offering in Montreal is so wide , so huge . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is remarkable also given &lt;a href= " https : **33;38080;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the long-term directorship of Nagano&lt;/a&gt; , which comes to an end , after 14 years , in August . Apparently Montrealers have not yet tired of the charismatic American . Schubert&amp;rsquo ; s Symphony No. 1 was rewarded with applause between movements , a sure sign that he still attracts newcomers . How the OSM will fare next season , when the throne is vacant , is a pressing question . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;38115;TOOLONG ... title= " Kent Nagano acknowledges the crowd at the start of the OSM 's free outdoor performance of Verdi 's Requiem at Olympic Park in August 2019. " alt= " Kent Nagano acknowledges the crowd at the start of the OSM 's free outdoor performance of Verdi 's Requiem at Olympic Park in August 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Christinne Muschi " **28;38150;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1715996 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Numbers are encouraging also at the OM , according to that orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s CEO , Jean Dupr&amp;eacute ; , who reports a 20 per cent increase in subscriptions over the last five years . He identifies the European tour of late 2017 as a breakthrough in consciousness-raising , although N&amp;eacute ; zet-S&amp;eacute ; guin , the native son who &lt;a href= " https : **33;38180;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;in September was made artistic director for life&lt;/a&gt; , has been leading the official opposition since he was appointed in 2000. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; More and more people are aware of the OM and what it is , what our culture is , how we present things , how different we are , &amp;rdquo ; Dupr&amp;eacute ; said last week , citing the case of a Yannick-led outdoor concert in July that drew an estimated 35,000 onlookers to the foot of Mount Royal . &amp;ldquo ; I think we are attracting Montrealers. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Similar multitudes converge on the outdoor OSM extravaganzas supervised by Nagano every August at Olympic Park . It is hard to believe that the picnickers who flock to such events differ significantly in their symphonic allegiances. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;38215;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;38250;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; In the Maison symphonique there might be some distinctions , although neither orchestra has recently made any systematic study of its consumer base . Dupr&amp;eacute ; , who makes a point of attending at least one OSM concert a year &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t tell anybody , but I do&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; sees a few general trends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In terms of age , it&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit younger , &amp;rdquo ; he says of the OM crowd , compared to the OSM. &amp;ldquo ; There are more families . People dress differently . &amp;rdquo ; That probably goes double for the outreach concerts in the boroughs and suburbs on which the OM has prided itself for decades . &amp;ldquo ; We are not a static orchestra , &amp;rdquo ; Dupr&amp;eacute ; adds . &amp;ldquo ; We are dynamic and moving . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some critics have judged the OM audience in the Maison symphonique to be quieter and more attentive , an opinion that is hard to reconcile with the public rebuke delivered by YNS to a restless Maison symphonique crowd in November . As for the OSM , there are certainly plenty of veteran concertgoers who suppress&amp;nbsp ; their coughs and leave their cellphones in their pockets , but Nagano&amp;rsquo ; s mysterious drawing power creates a&amp;nbsp ; heterogeneous mix . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;38286;TOOLONG ... title= " Yannick N ? zet-S ? guin is more than the local boy made good . He is the Montreal maestro gone international . " alt= " Yannick N ? zet-S ? guin is more than the local boy made good . He is the Montreal maestro gone international . " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;38321;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1716000 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The question of language is vexed . Ten years ago , this author wrote a column upbraiding anglos who &amp;ldquo ; don&amp;rsquo ; t know from Yannick. &amp;rdquo ; That was then . It would take a strange species of ostrich in 2020 not to be aware that &lt;a href= " https : **33;38351;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the music director of the Philadelphia Orchestra and the Metropolitan Opera lives in Montreal&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is tempting , but inadvisable , to resort to stereotypes . Perhaps the OM is young in orchestra years , but it is hardly a new ensemble , having been founded in 1981 by Conservatoire students who regarded the Orchestre symphonique de Montr&amp;eacute ; al ( which sported an alternative legal name , Montreal Symphony Orchestra , from 1954 to 1979 ) as a closed shop substantially populated by English-speaking musicians . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , Charles Dutoit&amp;rsquo ; s mother tongue was French , but the ultra-efficient Swiss toggled between languages in rehearsal according to the musicians being addressed or what lingo got the point across quicker . If this reality conflicted with the OSM&amp;rsquo ; s storied aptitude for French repertoire , so be it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; French was more clearly entrenched at the OM . The artistic directors following Marc B&amp;eacute ; langer &amp;mdash ; Agnes Grossmann and Joseph Rescigno &amp;mdash ; while not native French speakers , were quick studies . The arrival of N&amp;eacute ; zet-S&amp;eacute ; guin in 2000 left no doubt of the primary language of rehearsal , although OM players would be expected to work in English with guest conductors , notably as the usual pit band for the Op&amp;eacute ; ra de Montr&amp;eacute ; al . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No law prevents a music lover &amp;mdash ; French-speaking , English-speaking or Tagalog-speaking &amp;mdash ; from enjoying concerts by either orchestra . David Hutchinson , a retired software analyst , went so far as to subscribe to both before giving up on the OSM , partly as a protest against its withdrawal a few years ago of the seniors&amp;rsquo ; discount , which , as a boomer , he was looking forward to . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I like their underdog status &amp;mdash ; say , Concordia versus McGill &amp;mdash ; and the price was right , &amp;rdquo ; Hutchinson wrote in an email about the OM . The OSM was relying too much on &amp;ldquo ; gimmicky&amp;rdquo ; programming ( the OSM website at the moment is flogging &lt;a href= " https : **33;38386;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;a pair of Music , Fashion and Movement concerts&lt;/a&gt; featuring the input of the couturi&amp;egrave ; re Marie Saint Pierre ) . Hutchinson also welcomes the OM emphasis in 2019-20 on female guest conductors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Still , the consensus is that anglos are more likely to attend the OSM. &amp;ldquo ; I think this is a matter of history more than a matter of their having had the chance to be exposed to the OM , &amp;rdquo ; Dupr&amp;eacute ; says , while essentially confirming the hypothesis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Careau prefers to stress the multicultural character of the OSM crowd . &amp;ldquo ; We still have anglophones , but we also have allophones , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; For the past 15 years , maybe 20 , we have had a lot of immigrants , mostly speaking French , which is a world language . And the children of the anglophones are more French-speaking . They go to school in French . They are universal citizens . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Online and print communications from both orchestras are fastidiously bilingual , but it is noteworthy that OSM pops programming is now geared mostly to francophone listeners . The 2019-20 season opened officially on Sept. 17 with Nagano leading Shostakovich&amp;rsquo ; s Symphony No. 13 ( &amp;ldquo ; Babi Yar&amp;rdquo ; ) , but there were three concerts the previous week featuring the chanteuse Diane Dufresne. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;38421;TOOLONG ... title= " While the OSM throne will be vacant next season following the departure of Kent Nagano , chief executive Madeleine Careau believes the orchestra will survive the interregnum . " I am really confident about the programming . I have no doubt about this . " " alt= " While the OSM throne will be vacant next season following the departure of Kent Nagano , chief executive Madeleine Careau believes the orchestra will survive the interregnum . " I am really confident about the programming . I have no doubt about this . " " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;38456;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1715995 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If not quite apples and oranges , the ensembles are distinct varieties of apple . The OSM is a salaried orchestra with 92 permanent positions ( about a dozen of which are temporarily vacant ) . The 2019-20 minimum is $1,955 per week for a 46-week season ( for a total of $89,930 ) . Titled players , of which there are many , make more than the minimum . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The OM is a per-service orchestra with 58 musicians on its roster ( who are joined by extras in larger repertoire ) . There is a guaranteed minimum amount of work , which Dupr&amp;eacute ; says the orchestra inevitably surpasses by 50 per cent . Musicians are paid at a rate of $173.34 per service for OM presentations and the standard Guilde des musiciens rate for other events , for a typical annual total of about $32,000 . Many members of both orchestras work as teachers , the OSM players often at McGill and the OM players at the Universit&amp;eacute ; de Montr&amp;eacute ; al or the Conservatoire . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The OSM , with a 2018-19 budget of $37 million , has not only a bigger payroll but more tickets to sell . The OM ( with a 2018-19 budget of $7.1 million ) offers 16 events in the Maison symphonique in 2019-20 , including two concert performances of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s Fidelio in October that were co-produced by the Op&amp;eacute ; ra de Montr&amp;eacute ; al . The OSM has booked a dozen Maison symphonique concerts ( not counting recitals ) in February alone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The elder orchestra deploys a wider variety of programming , including recitals and concerts involving the Grand Orgue Pierre-B&amp;eacute ; ique . Happily for purists , the language problem limits OSM involvement in the Hollywood blockbuster business &amp;mdash ; the Toronto Symphony Orchestra in May offers its followers four opportunities to see Star Wars : The Force Awakens with live accompaniment &lt;em&gt; -- &lt;/em&gt; &amp;nbsp ; but leaves open the cinematic high road ( next month , Chaplin&amp;rsquo ; s silent classic&amp;nbsp ; The Gold Rush , with titles in English and French ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In some respects , the OM&amp;rsquo ; s underdog image affords it a PR advantage . Nagano led the OSM on a tour of the Americas in October , including two concerts at the famous Teatro Col&amp;oacute ; n in Buenos Aires , but Yannick and the OM got all the love in November for the orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s first performance in Carnegie Hall . We shall see to what extent Nagano&amp;rsquo ; s valedictory appearance in the New York temple as OSM music director on March 24 registers as an important event back home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;38486;TOOLONG ... title= " Kent Nagano 's directorship of the OSM began in 2006 . Ticket sales prove Montrealers have not tired of the charismatic American , who leaves his post in August . " alt= " Kent Nagano 's directorship of the OSM began in 2006 . Ticket sales prove Montrealers have not tired of the charismatic American , who leaves his post in August . " data-portal-copyright= " Phil Carpenter " **28;38521;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1715994 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both ensembles play ( and record ) at a high level under conductors who are not only capable musicians but helpfully distinct as podium personalities &amp;mdash ; the native Montrealer convivial and full of energy , the long-term visitor regal and given to philosophical pronouncements . While Nagano has a special relationship with the music of the 20th-century master Olivier Messiaen , it is fair to say both conductors feel an affinity for the core Austro-German classics . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For years it was assumed that the OSM was the truly global entity , but the OM acquired some of this cachet on its 2017 European tour ( which was financed in part by &lt;a href= " https : **33;38551;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the late Jacqueline Desmarais&lt;/a&gt; ) . A critic in Die Welt referred blithely to the OM as &amp;ldquo ; ein internationales **26;38586;TOOLONG ; ( a top international orchestra ) in the opening sentence of a review of a concert in Hamburg . Montreal critics also view the gap as narrower than it once was . &amp;ldquo ; Bigger is not necessarily better , &amp;rdquo ; wrote this author recently in a comparison of two renderings of Bruckner&amp;rsquo ; s Fourth , one more burnished , the other more urgent . ( You guess which. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The OM has fewer performances to play , but works hard in other ways . Unlike wind principals and associates in most established orchestras , the OM first chairs put in a full night&amp;rsquo ; s work rather than dividing duties between the first and second half of a concert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Size has other ramifications . The Schubert festival was possible only because the OSM can split into separate ensembles , each playing under the rubric of the OSM. &amp;nbsp ; There can be no such mitosis at the OM . Nor could the OM sustain such an extended festival under its music director , YNS being tied up with his activities in the United States and Europe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **33;38614;TOOLONG ... title= " " More and more people are aware of the OM and what it is , what our culture is , how we present things , how different we are , " says CEO Jean Dupr ? , citing the case of a N ? zet-S ? guin-led concert last July that drew an estimated 35,000 to the foot of Mount Royal . " alt= " " More and more people are aware of the OM and what it is , what our culture is , how we present things , how different we are , " says CEO Jean Dupr ? , citing the case of a N ? zet-S ? guin-led concert last July that drew an estimated 35,000 to the foot of Mount Royal . " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;38649;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1715999 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the imminent departure of its music director hardly bodes well for the OSM , Careau believes the orchestra will survive the interregnum , as it did from 2002 to 2006 , between the sudden resignation of Dutoit and the arrival of Nagano as music director . It says something about the durability of the OSM that the orchestra retained a following after the 2002 meltdown and was not much affected by &lt;a href= " https : **33;38679;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;the public allegations of sexual abuse levelled against Dutoit&lt;/a&gt; late in 2017. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There will be many attractive conductors and soloists , &amp;rdquo ; Careau says about the 2020-21 season , which will be announced in the middle of March . &amp;ldquo ; I am really confident about the programming . I have no doubt about this . The audience will be satisfied . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Nagano has already left the building as far as artistic planning is concerned , his international cabinet of advisers &amp;mdash ; Jean &lt;em&gt; -- &lt;/em&gt; Pierre Brossmann , Dieter Rexroth and Zarin Mehta &amp;mdash ; has joined director of music programming Marianne Perron in giving shape to 2020-21 . Expect artists-in-residence and mini-festivals of the Schubert type . Even in the symphonic sphere , Montreal is a festival-friendly town . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is also a city of music lovers . Nowhere else in North America can you find two symphony orchestras ( as opposed to chamber orchestras ) with touring schedules and substantial discographies led by conductors of international repute . Both enjoy government and corporate support . And if they attract different audiences , this dynamic can be taken as complementary rather than adversarial . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That is why I think there is room for two major orchestras in this city , &amp;rdquo ; Dupr&amp;eacute ; maintains . &amp;ldquo ; We can reach our own target markets . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another OM executive who did not wish to be identified put the matter as simply as it can be put : &amp;ldquo ; There is no loser in this comparison . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;38714;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;38756;TOOLONG ... N ? zet-S ? guin headlined a classic decade in **29;38791;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;38822;TOOLONG ... from Arthur **30;38857;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;38889;TOOLONG ...
